# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 033 ACID RAIN RUNTIME
# ============================================================================
# This file owns the single host-authoritative Event 033 transaction. Formation
# freezes one valid-state registry, scheduled host events process only bounded
# arrays, and every positive mortality path calls the shared exact population
# contract before any Event 33 receipt is written.
# ============================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Formation helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

acid_rain_assign_state_region = {
	clear_variable = acid_rain_region_id
	if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = europe }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.europe }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = middle_east }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.middle_east }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = asia }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.asia }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = africa }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.africa }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = australia }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.australia_pacific }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = north_america }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.north_america }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_on_continent = south_america }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region.south_america }
	}
}

acid_rain_initialize_region_arrays = {
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_target_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_observed_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_second_visit_observed_entries
	# Indexed writes need existing elements: size every region row before use.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_target_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_observed_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_second_visit_observed_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_target_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_observed_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_second_visit_observed_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initialize_region_arrays_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_initialize_region_arrays_loop_1_break
		start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
		end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
		value = acid_rain_region_index
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_region_state.untouched }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_target_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_observed_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_second_visit_observed_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	}
}

acid_rain_initialize_runtime_scalars = {
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_runtime_schema = constant:acid_rain_event.schema1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_generation_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { global.acid_rain_generation_id = constant:acid_rain_event.first_generation_id } }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_generation_id min = constant:acid_rain_event.first_generation_id max = constant:acid_rain_event.max_generation_id }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_runtime_start_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_start_chaos = global.chaos_meter_value }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.formation }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_active_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_completed_region_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_valid_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_deaths = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_prevented_deaths = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_exposure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_damage = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_air_applied_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_current_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_positive_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_positive_receipt_registry_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_damage_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_achievement_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_033_action_receipt_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_step = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_last_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_last_dissipation_roll = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_modifier_refresh_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.modifier_refresh_days }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_total_episodes = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_superstorm_opening_capacity }
	# The formation request covers the first week; the first weekly request is
	# due one week after formation.
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.contamination_days }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day = chaosx_date_offset }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_request_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_lifetime_added_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_complete_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_warning_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_transition_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_minimum_dissipation_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_progress = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day = global.date }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_transition_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_country_record_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_country_commitment_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_frozen_state_ids
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_region_chunks_indexed
	clear_variable = global.acid_rain_region_index_generation
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_applied_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_front_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_generation_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_front_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_generation_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_warning_front_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_warning_region_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_warning_generation_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_warning_day_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_ids
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_front_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_target_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_target_ids
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_ids
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_active_consequence_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_ids
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_target_count_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_cooldown_day_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_death_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_damage_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_low_preparedness_exposure_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_supply_disruption_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_count_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_episode_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_active_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_frontier
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_next_frontier
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_achievement_receipt_ids
	# Indexed writes need existing elements. Severe cells, superstorm slots and
	# warning visits use bounded ids, so every row is sized to its cap here;
	# an unused severe or superstorm slot reads as retired.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_retired = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_front_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_target_count_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_cooldown_day_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_death_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_damage_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_low_preparedness_exposure_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_supply_disruption_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries size = acid_rain_array_size value = acid_rain_array_retired }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_event.max_superstorms }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_count_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_episode_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries size = acid_rain_array_size value = acid_rain_array_retired }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_active_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_event.max_warnings }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_array_size = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_warning_front_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_warning_region_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_warning_generation_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_warning_day_entries size = acid_rain_array_size }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_death_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_damage_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_low_preparedness_exposure_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_supply_disruption_entries^0 = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Removes serialized prototype timer records without executing
# completion or timeout callbacks. The decision file retains inert identities
# so the native mission lookup resolves during the compatibility interval.
acid_rain_remove_legacy_missions = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = chaosx_acid_rain_timeout }
		remove_mission = chaosx_acid_rain_timeout
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = chaosx_acid_clouds_timeout }
		remove_mission = chaosx_acid_clouds_timeout
	}
}

# One-time legacy read. It removes the prototype modifiers and missions but
# only carries forward a state's observable legacy exposure as coverage; it
# never converts a prototype modifier into deaths or an invented loss receipt.
acid_rain_migrate_legacy_runtime = {
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_was_active = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_legacy_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain
				has_variable = global.acid_rain_continent
				has_variable = global.acid_clouds_time
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_receipt }
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_receipt
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_lock
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_was_active = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_legacy_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_rain_state }
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_clouds_state }
					}
				}
				CONTROLLER = { acid_rain_remove_legacy_missions = yes }
				OWNER = { acid_rain_remove_legacy_missions = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_rain_state }
				}
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_legacy_affected
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_legacy_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_rain_state }
			}
			# Regional cloud presentation is not evidence of acute state exposure.
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_clouds_state } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_clouds_state }
			}
		}
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain
		clear_variable = global.acid_rain_continent
		clear_variable = global.acid_clouds_time
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_continents
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Converts only states that carried a legacy exposure marker into
# frozen coverage receipts. A partial legacy region resumes on its second
# visit, while a fully marked region is closed immediately. If the old
# runtime had no recoverable valid state, the new runtime fails closed instead
# of creating a fresh event with ambiguous migration state.
acid_rain_adopt_legacy_coverage = {
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_recovered_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_migration_was_active value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_adopt_legacy_coverage_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_adopt_legacy_coverage_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_legacy_affected }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched } }
					set_state_flag = acid_rain_touched
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_state_coverage_receipt = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count }
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_states value = THIS }
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_front_entries value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_generation_entries value = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_exposure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				}
				set_variable = { acid_rain_target_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_acute_band = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.modifier_ordinary } acid_rain_set_acute_mitigated_values = yes add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_rain_state_ordinary days = global.acid_rain_modifier_refresh_days }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_migration_recovered_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				clr_state_flag = acid_rain_legacy_affected
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_adopt_legacy_coverage_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_adopt_legacy_coverage_loop_2_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_migration_region_id
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id value = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id compare = equals }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete compare = equals } }
				}
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_completed_region_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id value = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id compare = less_than }
				}
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_migration_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region_state.second_visit }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_migration_legacy_state_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_migration_recovered_state_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_failed_closed
		}
	}
}

acid_rain_form_frozen_registry = {
	acid_rain_initialize_region_arrays = yes
	every_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_frozen_valid
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_global_pulse_receipt
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_action_lock
				}
			}
			acid_rain_clear_state_runtime_receipts = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { acid_rain_state_is_valid_target = yes }
			acid_rain_assign_state_region = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_region_id }
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_frozen_valid
				set_variable = { acid_rain_registry_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_state_population_start_k = state_population_k }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_state_deaths = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_state_last_applied_loss = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_recovery_action_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_damage_pressure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_cursor = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				acid_rain_initialize_state_consequences = yes
				set_variable = { acid_rain_responsible_country_id = CONTROLLER.id }
				add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states value = THIS }
				add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_frozen_state_ids value = THIS.id }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = acid_rain_legacy_affected
				NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_frozen_valid }
			}
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_legacy_affected
		}
	}
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_valid_states = global.acid_rain_frozen_states^num }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_state_death_registry_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_form_frozen_registry_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_form_frozen_registry_loop_1_break
		start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
		end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
		value = acid_rain_region_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_index = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_completed_region_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
	}
	acid_rain_build_region_index = yes
	set_global_flag = acid_rain_registry_frozen
}

# ANY SCOPE. Builds the region index of the frozen registry: one array holding
# every frozen state grouped by region, each group in registry order, plus the
# half-open start and end position of every region's group. Region-bounded
# front, warning, retirement and severe-cell work reads only its region's group,
# so it visits the same states in the same order as a full registry scan.
acid_rain_build_region_index = {
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_region_chunks_indexed
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_index_start_entries
	clear_array = global.acid_rain_region_index_end_entries
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_index_array_size = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_index_start_entries size = acid_rain_index_array_size }
	resize_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_index_end_entries size = acid_rain_index_array_size }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_build_region_index_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_build_region_index_loop_1_break
		start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
		end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
		value = acid_rain_index_region
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_index_start_entries^acid_rain_index_region = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^num }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_index_next_chunk_start = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^num }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_build_region_index_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_build_region_index_loop_2_break
			array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_id value = acid_rain_index_region compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^num value = acid_rain_index_next_chunk_start compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks value = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^num }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_index_next_chunk_start = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^num }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_index_next_chunk_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
				}
				add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states value = THIS }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_index_end_entries^acid_rain_index_region = global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^num }
	}
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_index_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
	set_global_flag = acid_rain_region_chunks_indexed
}

# ANY SCOPE. Input: temporary acid_rain_cursor_region. Output: temporary
# acid_rain_cursor_start and acid_rain_cursor_end, the slice of
# global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states that holds that region's frozen
# states; an empty slice for region 0. The index is rebuilt first when it
# belongs to another generation, which also covers saves made before it existed.
# Callers declare both outputs before this nested helper returns.
acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_region_chunks_indexed }
				NOT = { has_variable = global.acid_rain_region_index_generation }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_index_generation value = global.acid_rain_generation_id compare = not_equals }
			}
		}
		acid_rain_build_region_index = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_cursor_region value = constant:acid_rain_region.first compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_cursor_region value = constant:acid_rain_region.last compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = global.acid_rain_region_index_start_entries^acid_rain_cursor_region }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = global.acid_rain_region_index_end_entries^acid_rain_cursor_region }
	}
}

acid_rain_initialize_country_record = {
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_registered
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_exposure_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_had_exposure
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_severe_forecast_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_warning_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_project_arrival_risk
	# Retired once-per-campaign impact gate of older saves; impact reports are keyed by episode.
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_severe_impact_reported
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_national_action_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_receipt
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_interrupted_cleanup
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_interrupted_stale_target
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_interrupted_control_change
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_preparation_receipt
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_project_shelter_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_project_water_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_project_medical_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_project_transport_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_shelter_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_water_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_medical_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_action_transport_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_medical_fatigue
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_recovery_tail_active
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_runtime_host
	set_variable = { acid_rain_preparedness = constant:acid_rain_preparedness.minimum }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_shelter_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_water_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_medical_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_transport_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_active_projects = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_committed_civilian_factories = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_committed_manpower = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.fragile_project_capacity }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_frozen_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_island_route = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_last_action_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuations_active = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuations_lifetime = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_active_exposed_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_current_warning_state_count = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_warning_state_count = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_aftermath_state_count = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
	clear_variable = acid_rain_water_protection_until
	clear_array = acid_rain_tier3_history_states
	set_variable = { acid_rain_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_exposure_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_start_population_people = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_prevented_deaths = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_estimate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_event_deaths = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_aftercare_completed_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_start_civilian_factories = num_of_civilian_factories }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_preparedness_peak = constant:acid_rain_preparedness.minimum }
	# The per-front warning key of older saves is retired; warnings are keyed by visit.
	clear_variable = acid_rain_last_warning_front_id
	clear_variable = acid_rain_last_warning_visit_id
	set_variable = { acid_rain_last_arrival_report_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_last_departure_report_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_last_casualty_report_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_last_casualty_threshold = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_last_preparation_report_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_pulse_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_aftercare_recovered_core_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_global_prepared_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_transport_reroute_receipts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_capital_route_checks = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_capital_route_isolation_receipts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_medical_fatigue_until = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_initialize_project_ledgers = yes
	acid_rain_initialize_action_quote_arrays = yes
	acid_rain_initialize_operation_rows = yes
	clear_variable = acid_rain_selected_project
	clear_variable = acid_rain_selected_tier
	clear_variable = acid_rain_selected_action
	clear_variable = acid_rain_first_opening_shock_day
	clear_variable = acid_rain_first_opening_shock_state_id
	clear_variable = acid_rain_last_severe_forecast_report_id
	clear_variable = acid_rain_last_superstorm_forecast_report_id
	clear_variable = acid_rain_last_severe_impact_report_id
	clear_variable = acid_rain_last_superstorm_impact_report_id
	clear_variable = acid_rain_exposure_start_day
	set_variable = { acid_rain_last_preparation_report_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_registered
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. A bounded controller-transfer hook creates a country record
# only when a frozen state changes responsibility. The original formation pass
# remains the authoritative baseline for every country already present.
acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = acid_rain_country_registered }
		}
		acid_rain_initialize_country_record = yes
		save_event_target_as = acid_rain_census_country
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes }
			add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k = state_population_k }
			set_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_start_population_people = event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k }
			multiply_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_start_population_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
			if = { limit = { is_island_state = yes } set_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_island_route = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one } }
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.local_state_limit compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.local_population_limit_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.regional_state_limit compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.regional_population_limit_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.national_state_limit compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.national_population_limit_k compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power }
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
		if = { limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_fragile_limit } set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_limited_limit } set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_established_limit } set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_band = acid_rain_country_geographic_band }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_band_cap = acid_rain_country_industrial_band }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_band_cap = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		clamp_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band min = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile max = acid_rain_country_band_cap }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.great_power_project_capacity } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.established_project_capacity } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.limited_project_capacity } }
		else = { set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.fragile_project_capacity } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
				num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.global_extra_factory_floor
			}
			add_to_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.extra_global_project_capacity }
		}
		acid_rain_recalculate_country_scale = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_country_record_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_countries compare = less_than } } add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries value = THIS } add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_country_record_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. The forecast flag is derived from current controlled targets,
# so one retiring state cannot clear a country that still controls another
# severe-cell or superstorm target.
acid_rain_refresh_country_forecast_status = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_severe_forecast_active
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_severe_forecast_active }
}

# STATE SCOPE. Ends a finished evacuation when its forecast episode closes:
# the evacuated flag and the evacuation disruption go, while the durable
# receipt and completion date remain for achievements.
acid_rain_close_state_evacuation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated }
			NOT = { hidden_trigger = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target } }
			NOT = { hidden_trigger = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target } }
		}
		clr_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = acid_rain_evacuation_disruption }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_end_day = global.date }
		clear_variable = acid_rain_evacuation_country_id
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Recalculates the decision scale from the country's current
# frozen-state responsibility without scanning any unrelated country.
acid_rain_recalculate_country_scale = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.local_state_limit compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.local_population_limit_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.regional_state_limit compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.regional_population_limit_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.national_state_limit compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.national_population_limit_k compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power }
	}
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
	if = { limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_fragile_limit } set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_limited_limit } set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_established_limit } set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power } }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_band = acid_rain_country_geographic_band }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_band_cap = acid_rain_country_industrial_band }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_band_cap = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
	clamp_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band min = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile max = acid_rain_country_band_cap }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.great_power_project_capacity } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.established_project_capacity } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.limited_project_capacity } }
	else = { set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.fragile_project_capacity } }
	# In-progress caps for evacuations and recovery operations follow the band.
	set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.evacuation_cap_fragile }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_recovery_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.recovery_cap_fragile }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.evacuation_cap_limited } set_variable = { acid_rain_recovery_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.recovery_cap_limited } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.evacuation_cap_established } set_variable = { acid_rain_recovery_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.recovery_cap_established } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power compare = equals } } set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.evacuation_cap_great_power } set_variable = { acid_rain_recovery_cap = constant:acid_rain_action.recovery_cap_great_power } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.global_extra_factory_floor
		}
		add_to_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.extra_global_project_capacity }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_island_route value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } } set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_island_heavy }
	else = { clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_island_heavy }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				cbrn_country_has_military_choking_protection_high = yes
				cbrn_chaos_warfare_has_advanced_protection = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_strong_cbrn_protection
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_strong_cbrn_protection }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_war = yes
			OR = {
				has_manpower < constant:acid_rain_ai_reserve.deployment_manpower_floor
				fuel_ratio < constant:acid_rain_ai_reserve.wartime_fuel_ratio_floor
				has_equipment = { support_equipment < constant:acid_rain_ai_reserve.wartime_support_floor }
				has_equipment = { train_equipment < constant:acid_rain_ai_reserve.wartime_train_floor }
				has_equipment = { convoy_1 < constant:acid_rain_ai_reserve.wartime_convoy_floor }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_wartime_reserve_pressure
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_wartime_reserve_pressure }
	acid_rain_refresh_country_project_quotes = yes
	acid_rain_refresh_country_action_quotes = yes
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Rebuilds only this country's responsibility counters from the
# frozen registry. This is idempotent across annexation bursts and reloads.
acid_rain_rebuild_country_responsibility = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_frozen_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_country_island_route = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	save_event_target_as = acid_rain_census_country
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = { acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes }
		add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k = acid_rain_state_population_start_k }
		if = { limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched } add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_touch_responsible_country_id = event_target:acid_rain_census_country.id }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_touch_responsibility_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_country_id = event_target:acid_rain_census_country.id }
		}
		if = { limit = { is_island_state = yes } set_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_island_route = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one } }
	}
	set_variable = { acid_rain_start_population_people = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k }
	multiply_variable = { acid_rain_start_population_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_active_exposed_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } } set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_exposure_active }
	else = { clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_exposure_active }
	if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_aftercare_unresolved } } set_country_flag = acid_rain_recovery_tail_active }
	else = { clr_country_flag = acid_rain_recovery_tail_active }
	acid_rain_refresh_country_forecast_status = yes
	acid_rain_recalculate_country_scale = yes
}

# STATE SCOPE. Called only for the changed frozen state. Active decisions are
# interrupted and refunded through their normal ledger, while historical death
# and damage totals remain with the country that received those exact receipts.
acid_rain_handle_state_control_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_aftermath_retained
			}
			has_event_target = acid_rain_transfer_new_controller
		}
		acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
		event_target:acid_rain_transfer_new_controller = { acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes }
		acid_rain_transfer_consequence_responsibility = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_transfer_state_id = THIS.id }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = acid_rain_action_lock
				has_variable = acid_rain_action_owner
			}
			var:acid_rain_action_owner = {
				set_variable = { acid_rain_selected_action_state = PREV }
				acid_rain_interrupt_state_operation = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_responsible_country_id = event_target:acid_rain_transfer_new_controller.id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		acid_rain_033_achievement_transfer_state_responsibility = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_country_id = event_target:acid_rain_transfer_new_controller.id }
		}
		event_target:acid_rain_transfer_new_controller = {
			acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
			acid_rain_rebuild_country_responsibility = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_transfer_old_controller }
			event_target:acid_rain_transfer_old_controller = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = acid_rain_country_registered }
					acid_rain_rebuild_country_responsibility = yes
				}
			}
		}
		acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
		CONTROLLER = { acid_rain_project_country_obligations = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_transfer_old_controller } event_target:acid_rain_transfer_old_controller = { acid_rain_project_country_obligations = yes } }
	}
}

acid_rain_form_country_registry = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			is_actual_nonhuman_country = no
			any_controlled_state = { acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_country_record_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_countries compare = less_than }
		}
		acid_rain_remove_legacy_missions = yes
		acid_rain_initialize_country_record = yes
		save_event_target_as = acid_rain_census_country
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes }
			add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k = state_population_k }
			if = {
				limit = { is_island_state = yes }
				set_variable = { event_target:acid_rain_census_country.acid_rain_country_island_route = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.local_state_limit compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.local_population_limit_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.regional_state_limit compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.regional_population_limit_k compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_states value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.national_state_limit compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k value = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.national_population_limit_k compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_geographic_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power }
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile }
		if = {
			limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_fragile_limit }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_limited_limit }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:acid_rain_country_scale.industrial_established_limit }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_country_industrial_band = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_band_cap = acid_rain_country_industrial_band }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_band_cap = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_country_band = acid_rain_country_geographic_band }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_country_band
			min = constant:acid_rain_country_band.fragile
			max = acid_rain_country_band_cap
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.great_power compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.great_power_project_capacity }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.established compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.established_project_capacity }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_band value = constant:acid_rain_country_band.limited compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.limited_project_capacity }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { acid_rain_project_capacity = constant:acid_rain_country_scale.fragile_project_capacity } }
		acid_rain_recalculate_country_scale = yes
		set_variable = { acid_rain_start_population_people = acid_rain_country_frozen_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { acid_rain_start_population_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
		acid_rain_033_achievement_begin_runtime_country = yes
		add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries value = THIS }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_country_record_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Settles one completed generation before an explicit later launch.
# Persistent migration history and shared Air contamination are intentionally
# excluded; every runtime, forecast, receipt, and presentation flag is reset.
acid_rain_reset_generation_flags = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_reset_generation_flags_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_reset_generation_flags_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
			acid_rain_cleanup_country_runtime = yes
		}
	}
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_endpoint_recorded
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_aftermath_retained
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_coverage_complete
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_final_dissipation_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_first_cross_region_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_formation_stage_committed
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_complete
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_host_bound
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_host_worker_scheduled
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_hyperacid_formation_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_lock
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_multiple_fronts_unlocked
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_receipt_overflow
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_registry_frozen
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_second_front_stable_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_damage_recorded
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_impact_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_unlocked
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_capacity_three_reached
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_capacity_four_reached
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_capacity_five_reached
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_third_front_resolved
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_third_front_stable_receipt
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_warning_registry_overflow
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_super_event_sent
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_formation_map_opened
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain
}

acid_rain_start_runtime = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active }
			acid_rain_launch_preflight_is_valid = yes
		}
		acid_rain_migrate_legacy_runtime = yes
		acid_rain_reset_generation_flags = yes
		acid_rain_initialize_runtime_scalars = yes
		acid_rain_initialize_front_arrays = yes
		acid_rain_form_frozen_registry = yes
		acid_rain_adopt_legacy_coverage = yes
		air_contamination_prepare_source_ledger_arrays = yes
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_lifetime_added_bp = global.air_contamination_source_lifetime_added_bp_entries^constant:acid_rain_air.source_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_current_bp = global.air_contamination_source_current_bp_entries^constant:acid_rain_air.source_id }
		set_variable = { global.air_contamination_acid_rain_bp = global.acid_rain_air_current_bp }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.air_contamination_bp } }
			set_variable = { global.air_contamination_bp = constant:air_contamination_source.zero }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_failed_closed } }
				acid_rain_form_country_registry = yes
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_start_runtime_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_start_runtime_loop_1_break
					array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
					acid_rain_033_achievement_register_runtime_state = yes
				}
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.regional }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_active_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { acid_rain_formation_has_global_opening = yes } }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^1 = constant:acid_rain_front_state.warning }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_generation_entries^1 = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_created_day_entries^1 = global.date }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					acid_rain_assign_next_front_region = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							acid_rain_formation_has_global_opening = yes
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_region_result value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
						}
					}
					set_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active
					set_global_flag = acid_rain
					set_global_flag = acid_rain_first_generation_started
					clr_global_flag = acid_rain_cluster_reserved
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { acid_rain_formation_has_global_opening = yes } }
						acid_rain_process_front = yes
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.formation_request_bp }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_request_is_formation = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					acid_rain_apply_air_request = yes
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_hyperacid_formation_receipt } }
						set_global_flag = acid_rain_hyperacid_formation_receipt
						set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.hyperacid_chaos_gain }
						add_chaos_meter_value = yes
					}
					acid_rain_bind_host_and_schedule_worker = yes
					acid_rain_gui_refresh_read_only = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^1 = constant:acid_rain_front_state.inactive }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.closed }
					set_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved
					set_global_flag = acid_rain_endpoint_recorded
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_endpoint_recorded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_migration_failed_closed }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_endpoint_recorded
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.closed }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Called by the root event after its formation super-event has
# been presented. It freezes the highest stage available at launch and enables
# every lower stage without adding evolution Chaos.
acid_rain_apply_formation_stage = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_formation_stage_committed }
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_formation_stage_committed
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_highest_stage_at_formation = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { acid_rain_severe_evolution_is_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_start_chaos value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_unlocked
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_highest_stage_at_formation = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_i_stage }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day = global.acid_rain_runtime_start_day }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_first_cell_formation_delay_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { acid_rain_multiple_fronts_evolution_is_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_start_chaos value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_two_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_highest_stage_at_formation = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_ii_stage }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { acid_rain_formation_has_global_opening = yes } }
				acid_rain_open_additional_front = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals } }
					set_global_flag = acid_rain_multiple_fronts_unlocked
				}
				else = {
					set_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_split_day = global.date }
					set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_split_notice_days }
					set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
					chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_split_day = chaosx_date_offset }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { acid_rain_global_evolution_is_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_start_chaos value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_highest_stage_at_formation = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_iii_stage }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.global_warning }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_warning_day = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_notice_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_warning_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.111 }
		}
	}
}

# END_EVENT033_CORE_PART_1

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Front selection, coverage, and ordinary pulses
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

acid_rain_calculate_front_goal = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_valid_count = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = acid_rain_first_visit_percent
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.first_visit_roll_min
			max = constant:acid_rain_runtime.first_visit_roll_max_exclusive
			integer = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal_unrounded = acid_rain_region_valid_count }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal_unrounded = acid_rain_first_visit_percent }
		divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal_unrounded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.percentage_divisor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal = acid_rain_front_goal_unrounded }
		round_temp_variable = acid_rain_front_goal
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_goal value = acid_rain_front_goal_unrounded compare = less_than } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_valid_count value = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_visit_min_states compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_goal min = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_visit_min_states max = acid_rain_region_valid_count }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal = acid_rain_region_valid_count } }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_target_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id = acid_rain_front_goal }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal = acid_rain_region_valid_count }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_goal = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_goal min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_region_valid_count }
	}
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_front_goal }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
}

# STATE SCOPE. Register the current controller and raise the exposure edge.
acid_rain_record_country_active_exposure = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_touch_controller_id = CONTROLLER.id }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_touch_is_new = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = acid_rain_touch_responsible_country_id
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_touch_responsible_country_id value = acid_rain_touch_controller_id compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_touch_responsibility_generation value = global.acid_rain_generation_id compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_country_touch_is_new = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { acid_rain_touch_responsible_country_id = acid_rain_touch_controller_id }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_touch_responsibility_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
	}
	save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
	# Arrival report facts: the front over the state, or the global layer at
	# the Standard intensity its pulses use.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_front = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			has_variable = acid_rain_target_front_id
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_front = acid_rain_target_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_intensity = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^acid_rain_target_front_id }
	}
	CONTROLLER = {
		acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_ready_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_arrival_report_day } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_ready_day = acid_rain_last_arrival_report_day } }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.report_cooldown_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_report_ready_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_active_exposed_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_033_achievement_last_runtime_check_day = global.date }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_exposure_start_day = global.date }
			set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_exposure_active
			set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_had_exposure
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_arrival_report_day }
						check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_arrival_report_ready_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_arrival_report_day = global.date }
				acid_rain_store_arrival_report_facts = yes
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.101 }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_country_touch_is_new value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { acid_rain_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
	}
}

# STATE SCOPE. Release one front target and clear the live exposure edge.
acid_rain_release_front_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target }
		acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_released_front_id = acid_rain_target_front_id }
		clr_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
		set_variable = { acid_rain_ordinary_inactive_day = global.date }
		remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states value = THIS }
		acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
		save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
		CONTROLLER = {
			acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_departure_report_ready_day = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_departure_report_day } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_departure_report_ready_day = acid_rain_last_departure_report_day } }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.report_cooldown_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_departure_report_ready_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_active_exposed_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
				}
				clr_country_flag = acid_rain_country_exposure_active
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_departure_report_day }
							check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_departure_report_ready_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { acid_rain_last_departure_report_day = global.date }
					acid_rain_store_departure_report_facts = yes
					country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.107 }
				}
			}
		}
		clr_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_released_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			subtract_from_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_released_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		clear_variable = acid_rain_target_front_id
		clear_variable = acid_rain_front_entry_day
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Stores the departure report facts just before the report is
# sent: the length of the national exposure that has just ended, the
# controlled states still carrying aftermath, and the highest aftermath tier
# among them. Only this country's controlled states are read, and only when
# some of them carry aftermath.
acid_rain_store_departure_report_facts = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_exposure_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_exposure_start_day }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = acid_rain_exposure_start_day }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_exposure_days = chaosx_date_days }
	}
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_aftermath_state_count = acid_rain_aftermath_state_count }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_departure_highest_tier = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_aftermath_state_count value = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero compare = greater_than } }
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = acid_rain_published_aftermath
				has_variable = acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier value = acid_rain_departure_highest_tier compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_departure_highest_tier = acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier }
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_highest_aftermath_tier = acid_rain_departure_highest_tier }
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Reports cumulative Event 33 deaths at 10,000, 100,000,
# 1,000,000, and then each full 5,000,000 deaths. The threshold ledger is
