# separate from the thirty-day report cooldown, so one report class cannot
# suppress forecast, impact, preparation, or departure reports.
acid_rain_update_casualty_report = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = acid_rain_event_deaths
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_event_deaths value = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_first_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_candidate = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_first_threshold }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_event_deaths value = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_second_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_candidate = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_second_threshold } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_event_deaths value = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_third_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_candidate = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_third_threshold } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_casualty_threshold }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_later_candidate = acid_rain_last_casualty_threshold }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_later_candidate = constant:acid_rain_reporting.casualty_later_step }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_event_deaths value = acid_rain_casualty_later_candidate compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_candidate = acid_rain_casualty_later_candidate } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_casualty_candidate value = acid_rain_last_casualty_threshold compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_event_deaths value = acid_rain_casualty_candidate compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_report_ready_day = acid_rain_last_casualty_report_day }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.report_cooldown_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_casualty_report_ready_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_casualty_report_ready_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_casualty_threshold = acid_rain_casualty_candidate }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_casualty_report_day = global.date }
				acid_rain_store_casualty_report_facts = yes
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.105 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. A preparation report requires both material parts of the
# authored counterfactual: at least 25,000 estimated prevented deaths and at
# least 25 percent lower applied loss than the same pulse path without
# protection. It is called only after a positive exact-loss receipt.
acid_rain_try_preparation_report = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = acid_rain_prevented_deaths
			has_variable = acid_rain_unprotected_estimate
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_prevented_deaths value = constant:acid_rain_reporting.prevented_deaths_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_preparation_ratio_threshold = acid_rain_unprotected_estimate }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_preparation_ratio_threshold = constant:acid_rain_reporting.prevented_loss_ratio }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_prevented_deaths value = acid_rain_preparation_ratio_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_preparedness value = constant:acid_rain_reporting.preparation_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_preparation_report_ready_day = acid_rain_last_preparation_report_day }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_reporting.preparation_cooldown_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_preparation_report_ready_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_preparation_report_ready_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_preparation_report_day = global.date }
				acid_rain_store_report_strongest_component = yes
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.106 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Computes the authored weight for one candidate region. The caller
# supplies acid_rain_region_candidate and the current stable front id.
# Output: caller-declared temporary acid_rain_region_candidate_weight.
acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete compare = equals } }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
			OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^1 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals } NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^2 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = equals } NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^3 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^1 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals } NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^2 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } }
			OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = equals } NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^3 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.base }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.untouched compare = equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.never_visited_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched value = acid_rain_highest_untouched_count compare = equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.highest_untouched_bonus } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_half = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate }
		divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_half = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.majority_divisor }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched value = acid_rain_region_candidate_half compare = greater_than } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.majority_untouched_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched value = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.large_untouched_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.fifty_or_more_bonus } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched value = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.medium_untouched_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.ten_to_forty_nine_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.current_region_multiplier } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_previous_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } } multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_region_weight.previous_region_multiplier } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate_untouched value = acid_rain_highest_untouched_count compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Selects one region through the explicit seven-entry weighted pool.
# A zero result means all unfinished regions are temporarily occupied or
# reserved by another stable front.
# Outputs: caller-declared acid_rain_region_candidate and
# acid_rain_region_weight_total. Internal candidate weights remain local.
acid_rain_select_weighted_region = {
	# Retire the saved scratch written by the earlier regular-variable retry.
	clear_variable = acid_rain_region_candidate
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_highest_untouched_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_select_weighted_region_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_select_weighted_region_loop_1_break
		start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
		end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
		value = acid_rain_region_scan_id
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_scan_untouched = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_scan_id }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_scan_untouched = global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_region_scan_id }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_scan_untouched value = acid_rain_highest_untouched_count compare = greater_than } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_highest_untouched_count = acid_rain_region_scan_untouched } }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_1 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_2 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_3 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.four }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_4 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.five }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_5 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.six }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_6 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.seven }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_compute_region_candidate_weight = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_7 = acid_rain_region_candidate_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_1 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_2 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_3 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_4 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_5 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_6 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = acid_rain_region_weight_7 }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_weight_total value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
		random_list = {
			acid_rain_region_weight_1 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one } }
			acid_rain_region_weight_2 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two } }
			acid_rain_region_weight_3 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three } }
			acid_rain_region_weight_4 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.four } }
			acid_rain_region_weight_5 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.five } }
			acid_rain_region_weight_6 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.six } }
			acid_rain_region_weight_7 = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.seven } }
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Prepares one front warning publication for its reports.
# Output temporary acid_rain_warning_report_key names the front visit: the
# allocated warning visit id, or, when the visit registry is full and that id
# is 0, a negative key built from the front id and the publication date so it
# never equals a visit id. Output acid_rain_warning_arrival_days counts the
# whole days until the front arrives.
# Inputs: acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id, acid_rain_front_id and
# acid_rain_warning_arrival_day (the stored arrival date).
acid_rain_prepare_warning_report = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_report_key = acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_report_key = acid_rain_front_id }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_report_key = constant:acid_rain_reporting.warning_fallback_front_stride }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_report_key = global.date }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_report_key = constant:acid_rain_runtime.negative_one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = acid_rain_warning_arrival_day }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_arrival_days = chaosx_date_days }
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Runs for every warned state the country controls during one
# front warning publication. The first state of a front visit stores the
# report facts and sends the advance warning; later states of the same visit
# only add to the warned-state count it shows, while a new visit by any
# front, including a return to the same region, warns again.
# Inputs: temporary acid_rain_warning_report_key, acid_rain_warning_arrival_days
# and acid_rain_selected_region_id.
acid_rain_send_front_warning_report = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_warning_visit_id }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_last_warning_visit_id value = acid_rain_warning_report_key compare = not_equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_warning_report_key }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_region_id = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_arrival_days = acid_rain_warning_arrival_days }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_warned_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_store_report_weakest_component = yes
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.102 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { acid_rain_report_warned_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Stores the lowest-tier Preparedness component for reports.
# Components are checked in Preparedness weight order, so a tie names the
# heavier one; 0 means every component has reached its last tier.
acid_rain_store_report_weakest_component = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_weakest_component = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_weakest_component_tier = constant:acid_rain_project.max_tier }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_shelter_tier value = acid_rain_weakest_component_tier compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_weakest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_shelter }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_weakest_component_tier = acid_rain_shelter_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_water_tier value = acid_rain_weakest_component_tier compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_weakest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_water }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_weakest_component_tier = acid_rain_water_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_medical_tier value = acid_rain_weakest_component_tier compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_weakest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_medical }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_weakest_component_tier = acid_rain_medical_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_transport_tier value = acid_rain_weakest_component_tier compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_weakest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_transport }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_weakest_component_tier = acid_rain_transport_tier }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Stores the highest-tier Preparedness component for the
# preparation report. Components are checked in Preparedness weight order and
# replaced only by a strictly higher tier, so a tie names the heavier one.
acid_rain_store_report_strongest_component = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_strongest_component = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_strongest_component_tier = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_shelter_tier value = acid_rain_strongest_component_tier compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_strongest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_shelter }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_strongest_component_tier = acid_rain_shelter_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_water_tier value = acid_rain_strongest_component_tier compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_strongest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_water }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_strongest_component_tier = acid_rain_water_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_medical_tier value = acid_rain_strongest_component_tier compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_strongest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_medical }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_strongest_component_tier = acid_rain_medical_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_transport_tier value = acid_rain_strongest_component_tier compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_strongest_component = constant:acid_rain_project.component_transport }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_strongest_component_tier = acid_rain_transport_tier }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Stores the arrival report facts just before the report is
# sent. Inputs: temporary acid_rain_arrival_report_front (0 under the global
# layer) and acid_rain_arrival_report_intensity. The urgent measures listed are
# the national operations that are not already running or recovering; their
# cost is checked by the decision itself.
acid_rain_store_arrival_report_facts = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_arrival_front = acid_rain_arrival_report_front }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_arrival_intensity = acid_rain_arrival_report_intensity }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_urgent_ready_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_report_shelter_ready
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_report_water_ready
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_report_medical_ready
	clr_country_flag = acid_rain_report_transport_ready
	if = {
		limit = { acid_rain_shelter_operation_available = yes }
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_report_shelter_ready
		add_to_variable = { acid_rain_report_urgent_ready_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { acid_rain_water_operation_available = yes }
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_report_water_ready
		add_to_variable = { acid_rain_report_urgent_ready_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { acid_rain_medical_operation_available = yes }
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_report_medical_ready
		add_to_variable = { acid_rain_report_urgent_ready_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { acid_rain_transport_operation_available = yes }
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_report_transport_ready
		add_to_variable = { acid_rain_report_urgent_ready_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Stores the casualty report facts just before the report is
# sent: the weakest Preparedness component and the exposed states the country
# controls but does not own, so the report never blames preparation alone
# when occupied territory carries part of the losses. The count reads the
# exposure each state already published for its controller.
acid_rain_store_casualty_report_facts = {
	acid_rain_store_report_weakest_component = yes
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_occupied_exposed_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = acid_rain_published_exposure
			NOT = { is_owned_by = PREV }
		}
		add_to_variable = { PREV.acid_rain_report_occupied_exposed_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Commits an immediately reserved region for a new front slot.
acid_rain_commit_front_region = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_previous_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_previous_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_previous_region_id }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_region_candidate }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_selected_region_id = acid_rain_region_candidate }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.first_visit compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.second_visit compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_region_state.second_visit }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one } }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_generation_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.warning }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.warning_days }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_previous_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
		# The first footprint is present at creation. Later destinations retain
		# their full warning lead and their existing arrival transaction.
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_empty_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard }
	acid_rain_calculate_front_goal = yes
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = acid_rain_footprint_percent
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.footprint_roll_min
			max = constant:acid_rain_runtime.footprint_roll_max_exclusive
			integer = yes
	}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded = acid_rain_footprint_percent }
		divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.percentage_divisor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal = acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded }
		round_temp_variable = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal value = acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded compare = less_than } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal
			min = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_min_states
			max = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_max_states
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal
			max = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_min_states compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		}
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_coverage_at_entry_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.acid_rain_total_touched_states }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_warning_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_warnings compare = less_than } }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = global.acid_rain_warning_count }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_front_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_front_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_region_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_generation_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
	}
	else = {
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_warning_registry_overflow
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_arrival_day = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
	acid_rain_prepare_warning_report = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_previous_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_previous_region_id value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = not_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_first_cross_region_receipt }
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_first_cross_region_receipt
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.cross_region_chaos_gain }
		add_chaos_meter_value = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_commit_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		break = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_break
		array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
		value = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start
		index = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_commit_cursor_end compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_commit_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_break
				start = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start
				end = acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end
				value = acid_rain_commit_cursor
				var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_commit_cursor = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							acid_rain_state_is_in_region = yes
							acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
						}
						set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_front_id = acid_rain_front_id }
						set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_region_id = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
						set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id }
						set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_day = global.date }
						set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_expiry_day = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.date compare = greater_than } }
							set_state_flag = acid_rain_warning_pending
							save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
							CONTROLLER = {
								acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
								set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_flag_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.warning_days }
								set_country_flag = { flag = acid_rain_country_warning_active days = acid_rain_warning_flag_days }
								set_country_flag = { flag = acid_rain_country_project_arrival_risk days = acid_rain_warning_flag_days }
								acid_rain_send_front_warning_report = yes
							}
						}
						else = { clr_state_flag = acid_rain_warning_pending }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_commit_front_region_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

acid_rain_assign_next_front_region = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_reassign_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		break = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_break
		array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
		value = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start
		index = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_reassign_cursor_end compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_reassign_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_break
				start = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_start
				end = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_end
				value = acid_rain_reassign_cursor
				var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_reassign_cursor = {
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals } } acid_rain_release_front_state = yes }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_attempts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_select_weighted_region = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
		acid_rain_commit_front_region = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_attempts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.max_target_attempts }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_2_break
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_region_result value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_attempts value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.max_target_attempts compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate min = constant:acid_rain_region.first max = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end integer = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^1 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } }
				}
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^2 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } }
				}
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = equals }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^3 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } }
				}
			}
			acid_rain_commit_front_region = yes
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_attempts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_region_result value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_weight_total value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_fallback_region_selected = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_assign_next_front_region_loop_3_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_region_candidate
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_fallback_region_selected value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete compare = equals } }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^1 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^2 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } }
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = equals }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^3 value = acid_rain_region_candidate compare = equals } }
					}
				}
				acid_rain_commit_front_region = yes
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_fallback_region_selected = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Freezes one visit's minimum and corrected maximum dwell dates.
acid_rain_initialize_front_visit_timing = {
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = acid_rain_front_minimum_dwell_days
		min = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.minimum_dwell_roll_min
		max = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.minimum_dwell_roll_max_exclusive
		integer = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days
		min = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.maximum_dwell_roll_min
		max = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.maximum_dwell_roll_max_exclusive
		integer = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.large_region_state_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.large_region_extra_days }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.high_intensity_reduction_days }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.forced_coverage_extra_days }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_visit_deadline_days_per_target_state }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded = acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days }
		round_temp_variable = acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded value = acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days compare = less_than } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.movement_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days value = acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days = acid_rain_first_visit_deadline_days_rounded }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days value = acid_rain_front_minimum_dwell_days compare = less_than_or_equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days = acid_rain_front_minimum_dwell_days }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.movement_days }
	}
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_min_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = acid_rain_front_minimum_dwell_days }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_min_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_max_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = acid_rain_front_maximum_dwell_days }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_max_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_coverage_at_entry_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.acid_rain_total_touched_states }
}

acid_rain_activate_front = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.warning compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_active_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_selected_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		acid_rain_initialize_front_visit_timing = yes
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Reveals and reserves one weighted destination while the current
# footprint remains live. The matching delayed host event executes the move;
# the bounded worker is an idempotent reload fallback.
acid_rain_prepare_front_warning = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_select_weighted_region = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_candidate = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_region_weight_total = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_select_weighted_region = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_candidate value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_selected_region_id = acid_rain_region_candidate }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_region_candidate }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.warning_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.retiring }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_warning_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_warnings compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = global.acid_rain_warning_count }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_front_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_front_id }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_region_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_generation_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		}
		else = {
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_warning_registry_overflow
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_arrival_day = global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		acid_rain_prepare_warning_report = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_break
			array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
			value = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_start
			index = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_warning_cursor_end compare = less_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_start }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_warning_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_loop_effect = {
					break = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_break
					start = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_start
					end = acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_end
					value = acid_rain_warning_cursor
					var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_warning_cursor = {
						if = {
							limit = { acid_rain_state_is_in_region = yes acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes }
							set_state_flag = acid_rain_warning_pending
							set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_front_id = acid_rain_front_id }
							set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_region_id = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
							set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_visit_id = acid_rain_current_warning_visit_id }
							set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_day = global.date }
							set_variable = { acid_rain_warning_expiry_day = global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
							acid_rain_033_achievement_record_runtime_warning = yes
							acid_rain_publish_state_country_counts = yes
							save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
							CONTROLLER = {
								acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
								set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_flag_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.warning_days }
								set_country_flag = { flag = acid_rain_country_warning_active days = acid_rain_warning_flag_days }
								set_country_flag = { flag = acid_rain_country_project_arrival_risk days = acid_rain_warning_flag_days }
								acid_rain_send_front_warning_report = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
		acid_rain_stamp_current_callback_chain = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_warning_event_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.warning_days }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.21 days = acid_rain_warning_event_days } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals } } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.22 days = acid_rain_warning_event_days } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = equals } } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.23 days = acid_rain_warning_event_days } }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.movement_retry_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Executes one already frozen destination transaction. Re-entry is
# guarded by the front state, generation, and nonzero reservation, making the
# delayed callback and worker fallback safe to race after a reload.
acid_rain_execute_front_move = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.retiring compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_generation_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_generation_id compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_move_coverage_gain = global.acid_rain_total_touched_states }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_move_coverage_gain = global.acid_rain_front_coverage_at_entry_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_movement_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_move_coverage_gain value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_zero_gain_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_zero_gain_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_zero_gain_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.correction_zero_gain_moves compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_movement_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.late_correction_movement_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_move_coverage_percent = global.acid_rain_total_touched_states }
			multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_move_coverage_percent = constant:acid_rain_runtime.percentage_divisor }
			divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_move_coverage_percent = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_move_coverage_percent value = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.late_correction_coverage_percent compare = less_than } }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_correction_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_departure_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_break
			array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
			value = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_start
			index = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_departure_cursor_end compare = less_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_start }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_departure_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_loop_effect = {
					break = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_break
					start = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_start
					end = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_end
					value = acid_rain_departure_cursor
					var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_departure_cursor = {
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals } } acid_rain_release_front_state = yes }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_previous_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_selected_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_previous_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_previous_region_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.first_visit compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.second_visit compare = equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_region_state.second_visit }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one } }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_generation_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		acid_rain_calculate_front_goal = yes
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = acid_rain_footprint_percent
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.footprint_roll_min
			max = constant:acid_rain_runtime.footprint_roll_max_exclusive
			integer = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded = acid_rain_footprint_percent }
		divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.percentage_divisor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal = acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded }
		round_temp_variable = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal value = acid_rain_initial_footprint_unrounded compare = less_than } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal min = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_min_states max = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_max_states }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal max = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_min_states compare = less_than } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal = global.acid_rain_region_valid_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id } }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_initial_footprint_goal }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
		acid_rain_initialize_front_visit_timing = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_arrival_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_break
			array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
			value = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_start
			index = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_start value = acid_rain_arrival_cursor_end compare = less_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_end = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_start }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_end max = acid_rain_arrival_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_loop_effect = {
					break = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_break
					start = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_start
					end = acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_end
					value = acid_rain_arrival_cursor
					var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_arrival_cursor = {
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_warning_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals } check_variable = { var = acid_rain_warning_region_id value = acid_rain_selected_region_id compare = equals } }
							acid_rain_033_achievement_record_runtime_arrival = yes
							clr_state_flag = acid_rain_warning_pending
							acid_rain_publish_state_country_counts = yes
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_execute_front_move_loop_2_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_previous_region_id value = acid_rain_selected_region_id compare = not_equals } NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_first_cross_region_receipt } }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_first_cross_region_receipt
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.cross_region_chaos_gain }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
		}
	}
}

# STATE SCOPE. One coverage receipt and one opening pulse are created for the
# state. `acid_rain_state_deaths` is always the exact helper output, never a
# requested estimate.
acid_rain_touch_state_for_front = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target }
		}
		acid_rain_open_ordinary_episode = yes
		set_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
		set_variable = { acid_rain_target_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_target_front_id = acid_rain_front_id }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_front_entry_day = global.date }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_pulse_day = global.date }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_state_arrival_visit_id = acid_rain_warning_visit_id }
		clr_state_flag = acid_rain_warning_pending
		acid_rain_record_country_active_exposure = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched } }
			set_state_flag = acid_rain_touched
			set_variable = { acid_rain_state_first_touch_day = global.date }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_state_coverage_receipt = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_states value = THIS }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_front_entries value = acid_rain_front_id }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_generation_entries value = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_exposure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states value = THIS } } } add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states value = THIS } }
		acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
		acid_rain_check_stable_fronts = yes
	}
}
# ANY SCOPE. Writes one bounded Event Log evolution row for Event 33. The
# caller owns the one-time receipt and supplies the accepted stage and tier.
acid_rain_record_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:acid_rain_event.id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = acid_rain_evolution_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = acid_rain_evolution_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host }
		event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
	}
	record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
}

# ANY SCOPE. A front becomes stable only after it has an active footprint with
# at least one touched state. The one-time direct Chaos outcomes are committed
# here, after both or all three fronts have independently proved that state.
acid_rain_check_stable_fronts = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_second_front_stable_receipt }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_stable_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_check_stable_fronts_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_check_stable_fronts_loop_1_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.front_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_stable_front_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_stable_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_stable_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_stable_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_stable_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_second_front_stable_receipt
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_two_chaos_gain }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evolution_stage = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_ii_stage }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evolution_tier = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_ii_tier }
			acid_rain_record_evolution = yes
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { news_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.202 } } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_third_front_stable_receipt }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_stable_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_check_stable_fronts_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_check_stable_fronts_loop_2_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.front_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_stable_front_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_stable_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_stable_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_stable_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_stable_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_third_front_stable_receipt
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_chaos_gain }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			# A third regional front is the same Multiple Weather Fronts stage,
			# already recorded at the stable second front. It is not Global Rain.
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { news_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.203 } } }
		}
	}
}

acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forced_state_found = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forced_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		break = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_break
		array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
		value = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_start
		index = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_forced_cursor_end compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_start }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_forced_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_break
				start = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_start
				end = acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_end
				value = acid_rain_forced_cursor
				var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_forced_cursor = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_forced_state_found value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
							acid_rain_state_is_in_region = yes
							NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched }
						}
						acid_rain_touch_state_for_front = yes
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forced_state_found = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Retires 20 to 40 percent of the mature footprint, keeping at
# least two live states. The first pass preserves states adjacent to untouched
# growth; the second pass is the bounded disconnected-island release path.
acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retired_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = acid_rain_front_retire_percent
			min = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.retire_percent_roll_min
			max = constant:acid_rain_front_timing.retire_percent_roll_max_exclusive
			integer = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_percent = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_visit_retire_percent }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_goal_unrounded = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_goal_unrounded = acid_rain_front_retire_percent }
		divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_goal_unrounded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.percentage_divisor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_goal = acid_rain_front_retire_goal_unrounded }
		round_temp_variable = acid_rain_front_retire_goal
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_retire_goal value = acid_rain_front_retire_goal_unrounded compare = greater_than } } subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_goal = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_maximum = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retire_maximum = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_retire_goal min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one max = acid_rain_front_retire_maximum }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_break
			array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
			value = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_start
			index = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_retire_cursor_end compare = less_than }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_start }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_retire_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
