				for_loop_effect = {
					break = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_break
					start = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_start
					end = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_end
					value = acid_rain_retire_cursor
					var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_retire_cursor = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
								check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals }
								has_variable = acid_rain_front_entry_day
								NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target }
								check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_retired_state_count value = acid_rain_front_retire_goal compare = less_than }
								NOT = { any_neighbor_state = { acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes check_variable = { var = acid_rain_region_id value = acid_rain_selected_region_id compare = equals } NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched } } }
							}
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_state_retire_day = acid_rain_front_entry_day }
							set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_coverage.retire_min_days }
							set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
							chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
							add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_state_retire_day = chaosx_date_offset }
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_front_state_retire_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
								acid_rain_release_front_state = yes
								add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retired_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_retired_state_count value = acid_rain_front_retire_goal compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_island_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_break
				array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
				value = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_start
				index = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_start value = acid_rain_retire_island_cursor_end compare = less_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_end = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_start }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
					clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_end max = acid_rain_retire_island_cursor_end }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						break = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_break
						start = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_start
						end = acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_end
						value = acid_rain_retire_island_cursor
						var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_retire_island_cursor = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
									check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals }
									has_variable = acid_rain_front_entry_day
									NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target }
									check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_retired_state_count value = acid_rain_front_retire_goal compare = less_than }
								}
								set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_state_retire_day = acid_rain_front_entry_day }
								set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_coverage.retire_min_days }
								set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
								chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
								add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_state_retire_day = chaosx_date_offset }
								if = {
									limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_front_state_retire_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
									acid_rain_release_front_state = yes
									add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_retired_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states_loop_2_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

acid_rain_process_front = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.warning compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_next_warning_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		acid_rain_activate_front = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_selected_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_visited_before = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = less_than }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_coverage.movement_batch }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id
				max = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_max_states
			}
			clamp_variable = {
				var = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id
				max = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } } acid_rain_retire_front_footprint_states = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_touch_limit = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_available_footprint = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_available_footprint = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_available_footprint min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_max_states }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_available_footprint = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_available_footprint value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = less_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_max_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_available_footprint = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_touch_limit = acid_rain_front_available_footprint }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_touch_limit max = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_connected_passes = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_connected_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		while_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_process_front_connected_break
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_front_touch_limit compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_connected_passes value = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_footprint_max_states compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_connected_before = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_touch_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_break
				array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
				value = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_start
				index = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_touch_cursor_end compare = less_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_start }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
					clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_touch_cursor_end }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						break = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_break
						start = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_start
						end = acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_end
						value = acid_rain_touch_cursor
						var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_touch_cursor = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									acid_rain_state_is_in_region = yes
									NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched }
									check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_front_touch_limit compare = less_than }
									OR = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } any_neighbor_state = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals } } }
								}
								acid_rain_touch_state_for_front = yes
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_connected_before compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_touch_island_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_break
					array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
					value = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_start
					index = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_index
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_start value = acid_rain_touch_island_cursor_end compare = less_than }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_end = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_start }
						add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
						clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_end max = acid_rain_touch_island_cursor_end }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
						for_loop_effect = {
							break = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_break
							start = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_start
							end = acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_end
							value = acid_rain_touch_island_cursor
							var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_touch_island_cursor = {
								if = {
									limit = {
										acid_rain_state_is_in_region = yes
										NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched }
										check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_front_touch_limit compare = less_than }
										check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_connected_before compare = equals }
									}
									acid_rain_touch_state_for_front = yes
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_2_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_connected_before compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_connected_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_connected_passes = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id
					value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_active_state_count_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = acid_rain_front_visited_before compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_empty_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_empty_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_empty_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_coverage.correction_after_empty_moves compare = greater_than_or_equals } check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = less_than } }
			acid_rain_force_lowest_uncovered_state = yes
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_empty_move_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.movement_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_next_work_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_min_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region_state.first_visit }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_first_visit_observed_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
			acid_rain_prepare_front_warning = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = global.acid_rain_front_target_goal_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_region_state.second_visit compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_min_departure_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_second_visit_observed_entries^acid_rain_selected_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_visited_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_completed_region_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			if = {
				limit = { acid_rain_all_regions_are_complete = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_selected_region_id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_complete_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_break
					array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
					value = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_start
					index = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_index
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_start value = acid_rain_complete_cursor_end compare = less_than }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_end = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_start }
						add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
						clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_end max = acid_rain_complete_cursor_end }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
						for_loop_effect = {
							break = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_break
							start = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_start
							end = acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_end
							value = acid_rain_complete_cursor
							var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_complete_cursor = {
								if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals } } acid_rain_release_front_state = yes }
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_front_loop_3_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						}
					}
				}
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.retired }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_active_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } } subtract_from_variable = { global.acid_rain_active_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
			}
			else = { acid_rain_prepare_front_warning = yes }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.retiring compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_move_day_entries^acid_rain_front_id compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		acid_rain_execute_front_move = yes
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact mortality, damage, and Air accounting
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# STATE SCOPE. Loads the exact Event 33 mortality profile for the current
# receipt. The request is rounded only after the formula, vulnerability,
# protection, pulse, and episode caps have all been applied.
acid_rain_calculate_mortality_profile = {
	acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cap_baseline_people = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cap_applied_people = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
	acid_rain_load_episode_cap_ledger = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_sustained_rate }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_sustained_cap }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_floor = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_protection_floor }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_episode_cap }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = acid_rain_pulse_class
				value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.ordinary
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_opening_rate }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_opening_cap }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = acid_rain_pulse_class
				value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_sustained_rate }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_sustained_cap }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_floor = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_protection_floor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_episode_cap }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_opening_rate }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_opening_cap }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = acid_rain_pulse_class
				value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.global
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.global_sustained_rate }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.global_sustained_cap }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_floor = constant:acid_rain_mortality.ordinary_protection_floor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.global_episode_cap }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.global_opening_rate }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.global_opening_cap }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = acid_rain_pulse_class
				value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.superstorm_sustained_rate }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.superstorm_sustained_cap }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_floor = constant:acid_rain_mortality.superstorm_protection_floor }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.global_episode_cap }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_rate = constant:acid_rain_mortality.superstorm_opening_rate }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.superstorm_opening_cap }
		}
	}

	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.base_vulnerability }
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.low_infrastructure_vulnerability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { rail_way < constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.damaged_transport_vulnerability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.supply_isolation_vulnerability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.dense_population_vulnerability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = OWNER } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.occupation_vulnerability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_history
				has_state_flag = acid_rain_aftercare_unresolved
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_local_vulnerability = constant:acid_rain_mortality.severe_history_vulnerability }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_local_vulnerability
		min = constant:acid_rain_mortality.base_vulnerability
		max = constant:acid_rain_mortality.maximum_vulnerability
	}

	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_wartime_exposure = constant:acid_rain_mortality.one }
	CONTROLLER = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_war = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_wartime_exposure = constant:acid_rain_mortality.wartime_exposure }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = OWNER } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_wartime_exposure = constant:acid_rain_mortality.occupied_exposure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_wartime_exposure = constant:acid_rain_mortality.combat_exposure }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_wartime_exposure
		min = constant:acid_rain_mortality.one
		max = constant:acid_rain_mortality.combat_exposure_cap
	}

	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_mortality.one }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_preparedness_for_pulse = constant:acid_rain_preparedness.minimum }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_shelter_tier_for_pulse = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_water_tier_for_pulse = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_medical_tier_for_pulse = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_transport_tier_for_pulse = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	CONTROLLER = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_preparedness }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_preparedness_for_pulse = acid_rain_preparedness }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_shelter_tier }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_shelter_tier_for_pulse = acid_rain_shelter_tier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_water_tier }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_water_tier_for_pulse = acid_rain_water_tier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_medical_tier }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_medical_tier_for_pulse = acid_rain_medical_tier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_transport_tier }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_transport_tier_for_pulse = acid_rain_transport_tier }
		}
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = {
		acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_mortality.protection_coefficient
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_preparedness_for_pulse }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection_reduction = acid_rain_protection }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_mortality.one }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_protection_reduction }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = acid_rain_shelter_tier_for_pulse }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.shelter_opening_tier_reduction }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_component_reduction }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = acid_rain_medical_tier_for_pulse }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.medical_sustained_tier_reduction }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_component_reduction }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.ordinary compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_window_days value = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.exposure_block_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = acid_rain_water_tier_for_pulse }
			multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.water_sustained_tier_reduction }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_component_reduction }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = acid_rain_transport_tier_for_pulse }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_component_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.transport_response_tier_reduction }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_component_reduction }
	}
	# Evacuation protects only against severe and superstorm pulses. A finished
	# evacuation gives the full reduction; one that has run a full day gives
	# the partial share while civilians are still moving.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_mortality.evacuation_protection }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { acid_rain_state_has_partial_evacuation = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_partial_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.evacuation_protection }
			multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_partial_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.evacuation_partial_share }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_evacuation_partial_reduction }
		}
	}
	CONTROLLER = {
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = acid_rain_action_shelter_active } subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_response.shelter_protection } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = acid_rain_action_water_active } subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_response.water_protection } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = acid_rain_action_medical_active } subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = constant:acid_rain_response.medical_protection } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = cbrn_civilian_mask_effective_coverage }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cbrn_reduction = cbrn_civilian_mask_effective_coverage }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cbrn_reduction = constant:acid_rain_mortality.cbrn_protection_coefficient }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protection = acid_rain_cbrn_reduction }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_protection
		min = acid_rain_pulse_floor
		max = constant:acid_rain_mortality.one
	}

	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = acid_rain_pulse_rate }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = acid_rain_pulse_intensity }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = acid_rain_local_vulnerability }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = acid_rain_wartime_exposure }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = acid_rain_protection }
	divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_requested_people = constant:acid_rain_mortality.thousand_per_million }

	# Pulse caps are population fractions of people, not thousands of people.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_state_cap = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_state_cap = constant:acid_rain_mortality.people_per_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_state_cap = acid_rain_pulse_cap }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people
		min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
		max = acid_rain_pulse_state_cap
	}

	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_remaining = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_cap_baseline_people value = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_remaining = acid_rain_cap_baseline_people }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_remaining = acid_rain_episode_cap }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_remaining = acid_rain_cap_applied_people }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_episode_remaining min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
			max = acid_rain_episode_remaining
		}
	}
	# Counterfactual estimate for reports only. It follows the same current
	# population, intensity, vulnerability, pulse cap, and episode cap, but
	# deliberately omits protection and never enters the population gateway.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = acid_rain_pulse_rate }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = acid_rain_pulse_intensity }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = acid_rain_local_vulnerability }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = acid_rain_wartime_exposure }
	divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = constant:acid_rain_mortality.thousand_per_million }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people
		min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
		max = acid_rain_pulse_state_cap
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_cap_baseline_people value = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_episode_remaining = acid_rain_cap_baseline_people }
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_episode_remaining = acid_rain_episode_cap }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_episode_remaining = acid_rain_cap_applied_people }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_unprotected_episode_remaining min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
			max = acid_rain_unprotected_episode_remaining
		}
	}
	# Match the shared helper's protected floor without calling it again.
	# Floor each availability before nearest-person rounding of the requests.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_floor_value = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_floor_value = constant:acid_rain_mortality.people_per_k }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_floor_value = constant:acid_rain_mortality.minimum_remaining_people }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_floor_value min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_floor_consequence_component = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_protected_available_people = acid_rain_floor_value }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_floor_value = acid_rain_pulse_state_cap }
	acid_rain_floor_consequence_component = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_state_cap = acid_rain_floor_value }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_floor_value = acid_rain_episode_remaining }
	acid_rain_floor_consequence_component = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_episode_remaining = acid_rain_floor_value }
	round_temp_variable = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people
	round_temp_variable = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_pulse_state_cap }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_pulse_state_cap }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_episode_remaining }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_episode_remaining }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_protected_available_people }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = acid_rain_protected_available_people }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_applied_request = acid_rain_pulse_requested_people }
}

# STATE SCOPE. This is the only Event 33 mortality gateway. A pulse key is
# written before the transaction, so a retry after save/load or a duplicate
# worker invocation cannot apply a second loss.
acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
			acid_rain_pulse_key_is_new = yes
			acid_rain_packet_has_current_layer = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_pulse_key = acid_rain_pulse_key }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.ordinary compare = equals }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_opening_pulse_recorded }
			}
			set_state_flag = acid_rain_opening_pulse_recorded
			set_variable = { acid_rain_first_opening_shock_day = global.date }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cbrn_snapshot_available = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		cbrn_refresh_state_civilian_mask_snapshot = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_applied_request = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_calculate_mortality_profile = yes
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_population_before_people = state_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { acid_rain_last_population_before_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_requested = acid_rain_pulse_applied_request }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_minimum_remaining = constant:acid_rain_mortality.minimum_remaining_people }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.acid_rain }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_log_deaths = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.one }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_target_country = CONTROLLER }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_has_target_country = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.one }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_contract_supplied = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.one }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_applied = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_result = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
		apply_exact_state_civilian_population_loss = yes
		set_variable = { acid_rain_state_last_applied_loss = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
		acid_rain_record_episode_applied_loss = yes
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_population_after_people = state_population_k }
		multiply_variable = { acid_rain_last_population_after_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_state_observed_loss = acid_rain_last_population_before_people }
		subtract_from_variable = { acid_rain_state_observed_loss = acid_rain_last_population_after_people }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_state_observed_loss
			min = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero
			max = acid_rain_last_population_before_people
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = acid_rain_state_observed_loss
					value = state_civilian_population_loss_applied
					compare = not_equals
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = acid_rain_population_contract_fault
		}
		acid_rain_033_achievement_record_runtime_pulse = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_opening value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			CONTROLLER = {
				acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_first_opening_shock_day } }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_first_opening_shock_day = global.date }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_first_opening_shock_state_id = PREV.id }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = state_civilian_population_loss_applied
					value = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { acid_rain_state_deaths = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_deaths = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_positive_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_humanitarian_amount = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_humanitarian_kind = constant:acid_rain_humanitarian.kind_deaths }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_humanitarian_revision = global.acid_rain_pulse_positive_receipt_count }
			acid_rain_register_humanitarian_pressure = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = global.acid_rain_positive_receipt_registry_count
						value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_positive_receipt_records
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_states index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.first_array_index }
				remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_applied_entries index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.first_array_index }
				remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_front_entries index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.first_array_index }
				remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_generation_entries index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.first_array_index }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_positive_receipt_registry_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_states value = THIS }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_applied_entries value = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_front_entries value = acid_rain_pulse_class }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_pulse_receipt_generation_entries value = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
			set_state_flag = acid_rain_positive_loss_receipt
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prevented_loss = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_prevented_loss = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_prevented_loss min = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_prevented_deaths = acid_rain_prevented_loss }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = acid_rain_severe_cell_id
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_death_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_id = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
			}
			CONTROLLER = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_event_deaths } }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_event_deaths = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero }
				}
				add_to_variable = { acid_rain_event_deaths = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
				add_to_variable = { acid_rain_prevented_deaths = acid_rain_prevented_loss }
				add_to_variable = { acid_rain_unprotected_estimate = acid_rain_unprotected_requested_people }
				acid_rain_update_casualty_report = yes
				acid_rain_try_preparation_report = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals }
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = state_civilian_population_loss_applied
					value = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.zero
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_episode_key = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_evacuation_status = constant:acid_rain_reporting.evacuation_none }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_end_day = global.date }
			acid_rain_prepare_severe_impact_report = yes
			CONTROLLER = {
				acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
				add_to_variable = { acid_rain_severe_pulse_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				acid_rain_send_severe_impact_report = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE SCOPE. Reads the facts of a positive severe or superstorm pulse for
# the impact report. Outputs (temporary): acid_rain_impact_episode_key, the
# severe cell id or the superstorm episode serial stamped at forecast;
# acid_rain_impact_evacuation_status, whether this state's evacuation had
# finished, was still under way with partial protection, or was absent; and
# acid_rain_impact_end_day, the stored end of the severe episode.
# Input: temporary acid_rain_pulse_class.
acid_rain_prepare_severe_impact_report = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_episode_key = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_end_day = global.date }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals }
			has_variable = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_id
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_episode_key = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_id }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_end_day } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_end_day = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_end_day } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
			has_variable = acid_rain_severe_cell_id
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_episode_key = acid_rain_severe_cell_id }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_severe_forecast_end_day } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_end_day = acid_rain_severe_forecast_end_day } }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_evacuation_status = constant:acid_rain_reporting.evacuation_none }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_evacuation_status = constant:acid_rain_reporting.evacuation_finished }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { acid_rain_state_has_partial_evacuation = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_impact_evacuation_status = constant:acid_rain_reporting.evacuation_partial }
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Sends the severe impact report after the first positive pulse
# of each severe episode in the country's territory. Severe cell ids and
# superstorm episode serials only grow within a generation, so a key above
# the stored one is a new episode; later pulses of the same episode, and of an
# older episode that overlaps a newer one, send nothing. Cells and superstorms
# keep separate keys because their serials are counted separately.
# Inputs: temporary acid_rain_pulse_class and the outputs of
# acid_rain_prepare_severe_impact_report.
acid_rain_send_severe_impact_report = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_impact_episode_key value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_superstorm_impact_report_id }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_last_superstorm_impact_report_id value = acid_rain_impact_episode_key compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_superstorm_impact_report_id = acid_rain_impact_episode_key }
				acid_rain_fire_severe_impact_report = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_severe_impact_report_id }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_last_severe_impact_report_id value = acid_rain_impact_episode_key compare = less_than }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { acid_rain_last_severe_impact_report_id = acid_rain_impact_episode_key }
			acid_rain_fire_severe_impact_report = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Stores the impact report facts and sends the report.
acid_rain_fire_severe_impact_report = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_impact_kind = acid_rain_pulse_class }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_evacuation_status = acid_rain_impact_evacuation_status }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = acid_rain_impact_end_day }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_episode_remaining_days = chaosx_date_days }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.104 }
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Opens the forecast report of a new severe cell or superstorm
# episode in the country's territory and sends it. The forecast loop then adds
# each of the country's forecast states through
# acid_rain_add_state_to_forecast_report, so the report counts them all and
# names the most populous one.
# Inputs: temporary acid_rain_forecast_report_kind (a mortality class) and
# acid_rain_forecast_report_lead_days.
acid_rain_open_forecast_report = {
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_kind = acid_rain_forecast_report_kind }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_lead_days = acid_rain_forecast_report_lead_days }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_top_population_k = constant:acid_rain_runtime.negative_one }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.103 }
	acid_rain_play_severe_forecast_cue = yes
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. Plays the thunder cue of sound/033_acid_rain_sound.asset for
# a human player whose territory has just received a severe cell or
# superstorm forecast, once per episode with the forecast report. The cue
# follows the player's super-event audio volume setting and stays silent at x0.
acid_rain_play_severe_forecast_cue = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			NOT = { check_variable = { settings_super_event_audio_volume = constant:settings_super_event_audio_volume.x0 } }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				scoped_sound_effect = "chaosx_acid_rain_severe_forecast_cue_[VOLUME_SUFFIX]"
			}
			VOLUME_SUFFIX = "[GetSuperEventAudioVolumeToken]"
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE, called from a forecast state's CONTROLLER block, so PREV is
# that state. Input: temporary acid_rain_forecast_state_population_k.
acid_rain_add_state_to_forecast_report = {
	add_to_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_report_forecast_top_population_k value = acid_rain_forecast_state_population_k compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_top_population_k = acid_rain_forecast_state_population_k }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_report_forecast_state = PREV }
	}
}

# STATE SCOPE. Building damage is a separate physical receipt and never a
# substitute for the exact population contract. Pressure is persistent on the
# frozen state, while each pulse spends at most its selected upper-band cap.
# Every spent point targets one real building level through a bounded cursor.
acid_rain_apply_state_damage = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
			acid_rain_packet_has_current_layer = yes
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_consequence_generation value = global.acid_rain_generation_id compare = equals }
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_last_damage_generation value = global.acid_rain_generation_id compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_key value = acid_rain_last_damage_pulse_key compare = greater_than }
			}
			OR = { NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_pulse_key } check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_key value = acid_rain_last_pulse_key compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_damage_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_consequence_window_id value = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero compare = equals } } acid_rain_start_consequence_window = yes }
		acid_rain_record_window_impact = yes
		set_variable = { acid_rain_last_damage_pulse_key = acid_rain_pulse_key }
		set_state_flag = acid_rain_state_damage_receipt
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_standard_min }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_standard_max }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_severe_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_severe_max }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_superstorm_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_superstorm_max }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.global compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_global_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_global_max }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_intensity value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_weak_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_weak_max }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_intensity value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_standard_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_standard_max }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_intensity value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_strong_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_strong_max }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_extreme_min }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_max = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_extreme_max }
		}
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = acid_rain_pressure_gain
			min = acid_rain_pressure_min
			max = acid_rain_pressure_max
			integer = no
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_protection = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		CONTROLLER = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_shelter_tier }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = acid_rain_shelter_tier }
				multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_component_reduction }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = acid_rain_pressure_component }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_water_tier }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = acid_rain_water_tier }
				multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_component_reduction }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = acid_rain_pressure_component }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_medical_tier }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = acid_rain_medical_tier }
				multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_component_reduction }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = acid_rain_pressure_component }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = acid_rain_transport_tier }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = acid_rain_transport_tier }
				multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_component_reduction }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = acid_rain_pressure_component }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = acid_rain_action_transport_active }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = constant:acid_rain_response.transport_damage_reduction }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_evacuated OR = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals } check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals } } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_evacuation_reduction }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = cbrn_civilian_mask_effective_coverage }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = cbrn_civilian_mask_effective_coverage }
			multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_component = constant:acid_rain_mortality.cbrn_protection_coefficient }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_reduction = acid_rain_pressure_component }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_protection = acid_rain_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_pressure_protection
			min = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.pressure_protection_floor
			max = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pressure_gain = acid_rain_pressure_protection }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_damage_pressure } }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_damage_pressure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		}
		add_to_variable = { acid_rain_damage_pressure = acid_rain_pressure_gain }
		set_variable = { acid_rain_state_damage_class = acid_rain_pulse_class }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_observed = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_points_spent = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_points_actual = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_points_cap = acid_rain_pressure_max }
		round_temp_variable = acid_rain_damage_points_cap
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_no_target = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_one = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = acid_rain_damage_one }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_apply_state_damage_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		while_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_apply_state_damage_loop_1_break
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_pressure value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_points_spent value = acid_rain_damage_points_cap compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_no_target value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = acid_rain_damage_one }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_budget_left = acid_rain_damage_points_cap }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_budget_left = acid_rain_damage_points_spent }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = dynamic_building_damage_amount max = acid_rain_damage_budget_left }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = dynamic_building_damage_amount max = acid_rain_damage_pressure }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_attempts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_applied = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_apply_state_damage_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			while_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_apply_state_damage_loop_2_break
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_attempts value = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.damage_target_loop_end compare = less_than }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_applied value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_attempts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor } }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_cursor = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				}
				add_to_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_cursor = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_air_damage.damage_target_loop_end compare = greater_than } }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_damage_target_cursor = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@infrastructure > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:infrastructure }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@rail_way > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:rail_way }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.three compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@industrial_complex > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:industrial_complex }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.four compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@arms_factory > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:arms_factory }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.five compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@air_base > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:air_base }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.six compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@naval_base > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:naval_base }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.seven compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@dockyard > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:dockyard }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.eight compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@anti_air_building > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:anti_air_building }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.nine compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@radar_station > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:radar_station }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.ten compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@infrastructure > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:infrastructure }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.eleven compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@rail_way > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:rail_way }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_cursor value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.twelve compare = equals } check_variable = { building_level@industrial_complex > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:industrial_complex }
					acid_rain_damage_selected_building_measured = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_target_applied value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
				subtract_from_variable = { acid_rain_damage_pressure = acid_rain_damage_observed }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_points_spent = acid_rain_damage_observed }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_points_actual = acid_rain_damage_observed }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_damage_no_target = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_damage = acid_rain_damage_points_actual }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_damage_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = acid_rain_severe_cell_id
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_points_actual value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_damage_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_id = acid_rain_damage_points_actual }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_damage_points_actual value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_pulse_class value = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_damage_recorded
				set_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_damage_recorded
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Central Event 33 Air gateway. The request is clamped before the
# shared Air ledger is called, then the actual applied delta returned by that
# ledger is the only value counted in Event 33 totals.
acid_rain_apply_air_request = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	# The shared source lifetime is authoritative across reloads and generations.
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_lifetime_added_bp = global.air_contamination_source_lifetime_added_bp_entries^constant:acid_rain_air.source_id }
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_air_can_receive_request = yes
			has_variable = acid_rain_air_requested_bp
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = acid_rain_air_request_is_formation
						value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			clamp_temp_variable = {
				var = acid_rain_air_requested_bp
				min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
				max = constant:acid_rain_air.weekly_max_bp
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_remaining_lifetime_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.lifetime_allowance_bp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = {
			acid_rain_air_remaining_lifetime_bp = global.acid_rain_air_lifetime_added_bp
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_remaining_ceiling_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.global_ceiling_bp }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_remaining_ceiling_bp = global.air_contamination_bp }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_remaining_lifetime_bp min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_remaining_ceiling_bp min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_air_requested_bp
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
			max = acid_rain_air_remaining_lifetime_bp
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_air_requested_bp
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
			max = acid_rain_air_remaining_ceiling_bp
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp = acid_rain_air_requested_bp }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.air_contamination_bp } }
				set_variable = { global.air_contamination_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.acid_rain_air_current_bp } }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_current_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { air_contamination_source_id = constant:acid_rain_air.source_id }
			set_temp_variable = { air_contamination_delta_bp = acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp }
			set_temp_variable = { air_contamination_source_current_bp = global.acid_rain_air_current_bp }
			add_to_temp_variable = { air_contamination_source_current_bp = air_contamination_delta_bp }
			air_contamination_apply_delta_bp = yes
			# The central effect replaces this caller-owned input with actual change.
			# Source-refresh scratch is overwritten while rebuilding other source rows.
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp = air_contamination_delta_bp }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_lifetime_added_bp = global.air_contamination_source_lifetime_added_bp_entries^constant:acid_rain_air.source_id }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_air_applied_bp = acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_current_after_bp = global.acid_rain_air_current_bp }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_current_after_bp = acid_rain_air_applied_delta_bp }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_current_bp = acid_rain_air_current_after_bp }
				set_variable = { global.air_contamination_acid_rain_bp = global.acid_rain_air_current_bp }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Weekly Air request from the acute weather on the map (Part 11).
# It runs in the regional and global phases, never while dissipation is
# pending, and is built from stored state only:
# - active world share: ordinary front targets plus active severe-cell states
#   outside them, or every valid state while the global layer is active,
#   divided by the valid states, in four bands
# - the average intensity band of the open fronts, or the global-layer band
# - a split adder for two or more open fronts and an adder for the global layer
# - a capped adder per active severe cell or superstorm
# A week without acute weather requests zero. Otherwise the request is clamped
# to the weekly range before acid_rain_apply_air_request applies the hard caps.
# The next due day advances one week from the previous due day, so the
# three-day worker keeps a seven-day average.
acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_phase value = constant:acid_rain_phase.regional compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_phase value = constant:acid_rain_phase.global compare = equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_states = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_states = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states^num }
			# A severe cell can outlast the front that formed it.
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_1_break
				array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target }
						has_variable = acid_rain_severe_cell_id
						check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_active_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_share = acid_rain_air_active_states }
			multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_share = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_percent_scale }
			divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_active_share = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states }
			if = {
