				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_active_share value = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_low compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_gain_low }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_active_share value = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_medium compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_gain_medium }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_active_share value = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_high compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_gain_high }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.active_share_gain_extreme } }
			# Average intensity of the open fronts; the global layer pulses at
			# the Standard intensity used by acid_rain_pulse_global_states.
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_open_fronts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_average_intensity = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_2_break
				start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
				end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.front_loop_end
				value = acid_rain_air_front_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_air_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_air_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.retiring compare = equals }
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_open_fronts = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_average_intensity = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^acid_rain_air_front_index }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_average_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_open_fronts value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				divide_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_average_intensity = acid_rain_air_open_fronts }
			}
			else = {
				# Only a severe cell whose front has moved on is raining; with
				# no open front to average, the lowest band applies.
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_average_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.weak }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_average_intensity value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.intensity_gain_extreme }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_average_intensity value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.intensity_gain_strong }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_average_intensity value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.intensity_gain_standard }
			}
			else = { add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.intensity_gain_weak } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_open_fronts value = constant:acid_rain_air.split_min_fronts compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.split_gain }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = constant:acid_rain_air.global_gain }
			}
			# Active severe cells and active superstorms add to one capped term.
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_3_break
				start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
				end = acid_rain_air_storm_loop_end
				value = acid_rain_air_severe_cell_index
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_air_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_superstorms }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_4_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request_loop_4_break
				start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
				end = acid_rain_air_storm_loop_end
				value = acid_rain_air_superstorm_index
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_air_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_gain = acid_rain_air_storm_count }
			multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_storm_gain = constant:acid_rain_air.severe_gain }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_storm_gain min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = constant:acid_rain_air.severe_gain_cap }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_requested_bp = acid_rain_air_storm_gain }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_requested_bp min = constant:acid_rain_air.weekly_min_bp max = constant:acid_rain_air.weekly_max_bp }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_request_bp = acid_rain_air_requested_bp }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_air_requested_bp value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_air_request_is_formation = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			acid_rain_apply_air_request = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.contamination_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		# A due day still not after today means no request ran for over a week,
		# as in a save made before this cadence; the cadence restarts from
		# today instead of replaying the missed weeks on consecutive ticks.
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day value = global.date compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day = global.date }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_air_week_start_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		}
	}
}

# END_EVENT033_MORTALITY_AND_AIR

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scheduled exposure workers and cumulative evolutions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# STATE SCOPE. The state is covered by the global layer once, even if an
# ordinary front previously visited it. Coverage receipts remain aligned with
# the frozen registry and the global pulse uses the same exact-loss gateway.
acid_rain_touch_state_for_global_layer = {
	if = {
		limit = { acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_touched } }
			set_state_flag = acid_rain_touched
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_touched_states = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_touched_entries^acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_state_coverage_receipt = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_count }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_states value = THIS }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_front_entries value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_coverage_receipt_generation_entries value = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_total_exposure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_global_pulse_receipt } }
			set_state_flag = acid_rain_global_pulse_receipt
			set_variable = { acid_rain_target_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_last_pulse_day = global.date }
			acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_consequence_window_id value = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero compare = equals } } acid_rain_start_consequence_window = yes }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_global_cap_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_global_cap_population_people = state_population_k }
			multiply_variable = { acid_rain_global_cap_population_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
			set_variable = { acid_rain_global_episode_loss = constant:acid_rain_aftermath.zero }
			acid_rain_record_country_active_exposure = yes
			acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Global-sustained pulses share the frozen registry and the exact
# population gateway, but retain a distinct phase receipt from regional fronts.
acid_rain_pulse_global_states = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_opening_pending_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_global_states_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_pulse_global_states_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
			if = {
				limit = {
					acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
					has_state_flag = acid_rain_global_pulse_receipt
					check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_last_pulse_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_next_pulse_day = acid_rain_last_pulse_day }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.exposure_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_next_pulse_day = chaosx_date_offset }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_global_next_pulse_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_last_pulse_day = global.date }
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.global }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_pulse_recorded } }
						set_state_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_pulse_recorded
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard }
					acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
					acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_pulse_recorded } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_opening_pending_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_complete }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_global_opening_pending_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_complete
			acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast = yes
			acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host }
				event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = {
					country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.112 }
					news_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.204 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE SCOPE. Repeated ordinary pulses are keyed by the shared generation
# sequence and are only processed for an independently open front.
acid_rain_pulse_front_states = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_front_is_open = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_front_states_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_pulse_front_states_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states
			if = {
				limit = {
					acid_rain_state_is_ordinary_pulse_target = yes
					check_variable = {
						var = acid_rain_target_front_id
						value = acid_rain_front_id
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = global.date
						value = acid_rain_last_pulse_day
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_next_pulse_day = acid_rain_last_pulse_day }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.exposure_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_next_pulse_day = chaosx_date_offset }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_next_pulse_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
					set_variable = { acid_rain_last_pulse_day = global.date }
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.ordinary }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_opening_pulse_recorded } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
					acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
					acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE SCOPE. Appends the current state once per integer target-weight point.
# The bounded repeated-scope pool gives `random_scope_in_array` an inspectable
# roulette distribution while keeping active-front geometry as the hard gate.
acid_rain_append_state_to_severe_weighted_pool = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.base }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_population_start_k value = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.population_medium_k compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.population_medium_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_population_start_k value = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.population_large_k compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.population_large_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_population_start_k value = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.population_dense_k compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.population_dense_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.logistics_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_opening_pulse_recorded }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.prior_exposure_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { CONTROLLER = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_preparedness value = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.low_preparedness_threshold compare = less_than } } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.low_preparedness_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { CONTROLLER = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_preparedness value = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.moderate_preparedness_threshold compare = less_than } } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.moderate_preparedness_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_history }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.previous_severe_penalty }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_severe_state_weight
		min = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.minimum
		max = constant:acid_rain_severe_target_weight.maximum
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_append_state_to_severe_weighted_pool_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_append_state_to_severe_weighted_pool_loop_1_break
		limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
		add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates value = THIS }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Selects and activates one bounded severe cell. Every pick uses a
# weighted active-footprint pool; adjacency is required while a connected
# candidate exists, then a second bounded pass admits disconnected islands.
acid_rain_create_severe_cell = {
	# Every state this front targets lies in its current region, so each pool
	# scan reads only that region's slice of the frozen registry.
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_front_region = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_active_front_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_severe_front_region }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_count_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_each_loop = {
		break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_break
		array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
		value = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_start
		index = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_start value = acid_rain_severe_count_cursor_end compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_start }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_end max = acid_rain_severe_count_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_break
				start = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_start
				end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_end
				value = acid_rain_severe_count_cursor
				var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_severe_count_cursor = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals }
						}
						add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_active_front_state_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_1_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_active_front_state_count value = constant:acid_rain_coverage.first_visit_min_states compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells compare = less_than }
		}
		set_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_severe_target_goal min = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_min_target_states max = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_max_target_states_exclusive integer = yes }
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_frontier
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_next_frontier
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_target_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		while_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_2_break
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets^num value = acid_rain_severe_target_goal compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_target_attempt value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.max_target_attempts compare = less_than }
			}
			clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_severe_front_region }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_pool_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_break
				array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
				value = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_start
				index = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_start value = acid_rain_severe_pool_cursor_end compare = less_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_start }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
					clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_end max = acid_rain_severe_pool_cursor_end }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_break
						start = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_start
						end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_end
						value = acid_rain_severe_pool_cursor
						var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_severe_pool_cursor = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
									check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals }
									NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target }
									NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS } }
									OR = {
										check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
										any_neighbor_state = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS } }
									}
								}
								acid_rain_append_state_to_severe_weighted_pool = yes
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_3_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
				# No connected candidate remains. Rebuild the same weighted pool
				# without adjacency so a disconnected island can be selected.
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_severe_front_region }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_island_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_break
					array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
					value = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_start
					index = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_index
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_start value = acid_rain_severe_island_cursor_end compare = less_than }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_start }
						add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
						clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_end max = acid_rain_severe_island_cursor_end }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
						for_loop_effect = {
							break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_break
							start = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_start
							end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_end
							value = acid_rain_severe_island_cursor
							var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_severe_island_cursor = {
								if = {
									limit = {
										acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
										check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals }
										NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target }
										NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS } }
									}
									acid_rain_append_state_to_severe_weighted_pool = yes
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_4_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				random_scope_in_array = {
					array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates
					limit = {
						acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS } }
					}
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS }
				}
			}
			else = {
				# Force the bounded selector to its terminal state when the
				# active footprint has no remaining eligible state.
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_target_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.max_target_attempts }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_target_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets^num value = acid_rain_severe_target_goal compare = less_than } }
			clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_severe_front_region }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_fill_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_break
				array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
				value = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_start
				index = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_start value = acid_rain_severe_fill_cursor_end compare = less_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_start }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
					clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_end max = acid_rain_severe_fill_cursor_end }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_break
						start = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_start
						end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_end
						value = acid_rain_severe_fill_cursor
						var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_severe_fill_cursor = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
									check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_front_id compare = equals }
									NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target }
									NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS } }
								}
								acid_rain_append_state_to_severe_weighted_pool = yes
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_5_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				random_scope_in_array = {
					array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_weighted_candidates
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS } } }
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets value = THIS }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets^num value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_min_target_states compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count }
			add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_ids value = acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_front_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = acid_rain_front_id }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_forecast_lead_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = chaosx_date_offset }
			set_temp_variable_to_random = { var = chaosx_date_days min = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_duration_min_days max = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_duration_max_days_exclusive integer = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = chaosx_date_offset }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_target_count_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets^num }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_cooldown_day_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_death_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_damage_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_low_preparedness_exposure_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_supply_disruption_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_6_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_6_break
				array = global.acid_rain_severe_planner_targets
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_forecast_pending
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_forecast_reported
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_id = acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
				clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_episode_impacted
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_id = acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_day = global.date }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_start_day = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
				# An evacuation started after this day cannot finish before impact.
				set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_latest_start_day = acid_rain_severe_forecast_start_day }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_urgent_cost.evacuation_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				subtract_from_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_latest_start_day = chaosx_date_offset }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_end_day = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_severe_pulse_day = global.date }
				set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_opening_recorded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				acid_rain_033_achievement_record_runtime_forecast = yes
				save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forecast_state_population_k = state_population_k }
				CONTROLLER = {
					acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
					set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_severe_forecast_active
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_severe_forecast_report_id }
								check_variable = { var = acid_rain_last_severe_forecast_report_id value = acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id compare = not_equals }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { acid_rain_last_severe_forecast_report_id = acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id }
						add_to_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forecast_report_kind = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forecast_report_lead_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_forecast_lead_days }
						acid_rain_open_forecast_report = yes
					}
					acid_rain_add_state_to_forecast_report = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { CONTROLLER = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_preparedness value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_low_preparedness_threshold compare = less_than } } }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_low_preparedness_exposed_people = acid_rain_state_population_start_k }
					multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_low_preparedness_exposed_people = constant:chaos_meter_deaths.people_per_k }
					add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_low_preparedness_exposure_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = acid_rain_low_preparedness_exposed_people }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes }
					set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_supply_disruption_entries^acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				}
				acid_rain_publish_state_country_counts = yes
			}
			# Preserve original frozen-state processing order when publishing the committed target registry.
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_region = acid_rain_severe_front_region }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_cursor_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			acid_rain_prepare_region_cursor = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_publish_cursor_end = acid_rain_cursor_end }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_break
				array = global.acid_rain_region_iteration_chunks
				value = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_start
				index = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_start value = acid_rain_cursor_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_start value = acid_rain_severe_publish_cursor_end compare = less_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_start }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_cursor_batch_size }
					clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_end max = acid_rain_severe_publish_cursor_end }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						break = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_break
						start = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_start
						end = acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_end
						value = acid_rain_severe_publish_cursor
						var:global.acid_rain_region_ordered_states^acid_rain_severe_publish_cursor = {
							if = { limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_cell_id value = acid_rain_new_severe_cell_id compare = equals } NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states value = THIS } } } add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states value = THIS } }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_break > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_severe_cell_loop_7_chunk_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Rising Chaos unlocks the severe-cell system, but the unlock itself
# has no Chaos effect. Only an actually activated cell receives the mapped
# direct +5 change in the processing helper below.
acid_rain_try_severe_evolution = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending NOT = { acid_rain_severe_evolution_is_enabled = yes } }
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending
		clear_variable = global.acid_rain_severe_transition_day
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_severe_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_runtime_start_day value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_unlocked }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_transition_day = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_notice_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_transition_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.114 } } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending
			acid_rain_severe_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_severe_transition_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_unlocked
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day = global.date }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.acid_rain_runtime_start_day }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_age_days = chaosx_date_days }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_minimum_start_age = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_minimum_age_days }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_start_chaos value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_minimum_start_age = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_first_cell_formation_delay_days }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_unlocked
			acid_rain_severe_evolution_is_not_disabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_age_days value = acid_rain_severe_minimum_start_age compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_base_chance }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos_500_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos_500_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^1 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^2 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^3 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_strong_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^1 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^2 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^3 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_extreme_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_age_days value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_age_30_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_age_30_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_age_days value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_age_45_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_age_45_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_previous_cell_penalty }
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_severe_chance
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
			max = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chance_cap
		}
		set_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_severe_roll min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one max = constant:acid_rain_event.probability_roll_max_exclusive integer = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_roll value = acid_rain_severe_chance compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_front_selected = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_severe_evolution_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_try_severe_evolution_loop_1_break
				start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
				end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.front_loop_end
				value = acid_rain_severe_front_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_front_selected value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_severe_front_index value = constant:acid_rain_front_state.active compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_severe_cooldown_day_entries^acid_rain_severe_front_index compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_front_has_active_cell = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_severe_evolution_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						break = acid_rain_try_severe_evolution_loop_2_break
						start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
						end = acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end
						value = acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
								check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_front_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index value = acid_rain_severe_front_index compare = equals }
							}
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_front_has_active_cell = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_front_has_active_cell value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_front_selected = acid_rain_severe_front_index }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_front_selected value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_severe_front_selected }
				acid_rain_create_severe_cell = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_evaluation_cadence_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_next_check_day = chaosx_date_offset }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. The first qualifying severe-cell impact commits one direct Chaos
# receipt. It may be the first or a later cell, but the receipt is global and
# never fires for a forecast that remains below every accepted threshold.
acid_rain_try_severe_impact_chaos = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_impact_receipt }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_impact_found = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_severe_impact_chaos_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_try_severe_impact_chaos_loop_1_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
			end = acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_impact_found value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_death_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos_receipt_deaths compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_damage_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos_receipt_damage compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						AND = {
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_low_preparedness_exposure_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos_receipt_exposed compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_supply_disruption_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_impact_found = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_impact_found value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_impact_receipt
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_chaos_gain }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evolution_stage = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_i_stage }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evolution_tier = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_i_tier }
			acid_rain_record_evolution = yes
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.108 } } }
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Severe target processing remains cumulative with ordinary fronts.
# A cell starts after its forecast notice, receives its own exact class pulses,
# and retires without deleting the durable severe-history receipt.
acid_rain_process_severe_cells = {
	clear_temp_array = acid_rain_severe_processing_snapshot
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_each_scope_loop = { break = acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_1_break array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states add_to_temp_array = { array = acid_rain_severe_processing_snapshot value = THIS } }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_2_break
		start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
		end = acid_rain_severe_cell_loop_end
		value = acid_rain_severe_cell_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count compare = less_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_retiring_status = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_4_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_4_break
				array = acid_rain_severe_processing_snapshot
				if = {
					limit = { acid_rain_state_is_severe_worker_owned = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_retiring_status value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
						acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
						acid_rain_033_achievement_close_runtime_episode = yes
					}
					clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target
					remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states value = THIS }
					clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_forecast_pending
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_retiring_status value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_episode_impacted } set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_history }
					acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
					acid_rain_close_state_evacuation = yes
					CONTROLLER = { acid_rain_refresh_country_forecast_status = yes }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_retiring_status value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_cooldown_day_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index = global.date }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_front_id = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_front_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index }
				set_temp_variable_to_random = { var = chaosx_date_days min = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_cooldown_min_days max = constant:acid_rain_evolution.severe_cooldown_max_days_exclusive integer = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_severe_cooldown_day_entries^acid_rain_severe_front_id = global.date }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_severe_cooldown_day_entries^acid_rain_severe_front_id = chaosx_date_offset }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_count compare = less_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_start_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = global.date compare = less_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_end_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index compare = less_than }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_process_severe_cells_loop_3_break
				array = acid_rain_severe_processing_snapshot
				if = {
					limit = { acid_rain_state_is_severe_worker_owned = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
						set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_severe_cell_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_severe_cell_opening_recorded }
								check_variable = { var = acid_rain_severe_cell_opening_recorded value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { acid_rain_last_severe_pulse_day = global.date }
						set_variable = { acid_rain_severe_cell_opening_recorded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong }
						acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
						acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_next_pulse_day = acid_rain_last_severe_pulse_day }
						set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.exposure_days }
						set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
						chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
						add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_severe_next_pulse_day = chaosx_date_offset }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_severe_next_pulse_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
							set_variable = { acid_rain_last_severe_pulse_day = global.date }
							add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.severe }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong }
							acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
							acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	acid_rain_try_severe_impact_chaos = yes
}

# END_EVENT033_EVOLUTIONS_PART_1

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Additional fronts and the global layer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ANY SCOPE. Opens a new independent front only after the bounded region
# selector has proved a distinct untouched region. A failed selection does not
# consume a front slot or a mapped Chaos change.
acid_rain_open_additional_front = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_new_front_id = global.acid_rain_front_count }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_new_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_new_front_id value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_fronts compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.warning }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_visit_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_generation_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_created_day_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = global.date }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_initial_footprint_done_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_new_front_id }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_region_result = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		acid_rain_assign_next_front_region = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_front_region_result value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_count = acid_rain_new_front_id }
			acid_rain_process_front = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_new_front_id = constant:acid_rain_front_state.inactive }
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Chaos Tier opens a second front deterministically. The third
# front is a capped probability roll and retries after sixty days when no
# distinct region is available; neither route shares front state or warnings.
acid_rain_try_multiple_fronts = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending NOT = { acid_rain_multiple_fronts_evolution_is_enabled = yes } }
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
		clear_variable = global.acid_rain_front_split_day
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_multiple_fronts_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_two_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_multiple_fronts_unlocked }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_split_day = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_split_notice_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_split_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.113 } } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
			acid_rain_multiple_fronts_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_front_split_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		acid_rain_open_additional_front = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals } }
			clr_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_multiple_fronts_unlocked
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_split_day = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_split_notice_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_split_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_multiple_fronts_unlocked
			acid_rain_multiple_fronts_evolution_is_not_disabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_third_front_resolved }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_two_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.acid_rain_runtime_start_day }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_third_front_age_days = chaosx_date_days }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_third_front_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_base_chance }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_chaos_700_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_third_front_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_chaos_700_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^1 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_intensity_entries^2 value = constant:acid_rain_intensity.strong compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_third_front_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_intensity_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_third_front_age_days value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_age_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_third_front_chance = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_age_bonus }
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = acid_rain_third_front_chance
			min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero
			max = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_chance_cap
		}
		set_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_third_front_roll min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one max = constant:acid_rain_event.probability_roll_max_exclusive integer = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_third_front_roll value = acid_rain_third_front_chance compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_count_before = global.acid_rain_front_count }
			acid_rain_open_additional_front = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_front_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_fronts compare = equals } }
				set_global_flag = acid_rain_third_front_resolved
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day = global.date }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_retry_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.front_three_retry_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_third_front_retry_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Global-layer coverage uses a distinct receipt from front
# coverage but preserves the same frozen generation and region assignment.
acid_rain_begin_global_transition = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_runtime_has_frozen_registry = yes
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
			has_variable = global.acid_rain_global_warning_day
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_global_warning_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			all_of_scopes = {
				array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
				acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
			}
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
			acid_rain_recalculate_country_scale = yes
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.global }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_transition_day = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_2_break
			array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
			# The first global check follows all committed transition consequences.
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_minimum_dissipation_day = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_minimum_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_minimum_dissipation_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = global.acid_rain_global_minimum_dissipation_day }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_receipt
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_chaos_gain }
		add_chaos_meter_value = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evolution_stage = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_evolution_tier = constant:acid_rain_event.evolution_iii_tier }
		acid_rain_record_evolution = yes
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_completed_region_count = constant:acid_rain_region.count }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_3_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_global_region_index
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_region_state_entries^acid_rain_global_region_index = constant:acid_rain_region_state.complete }
		}
		# Regional severe cells are retired before global targets are created.
		# Their history remains, but no regional pulse can survive the phase change.
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_4_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_4_break
			array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target }
				acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
				acid_rain_assess_window_aftermath = yes
				acid_rain_033_achievement_close_runtime_episode = yes
				clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target
				clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_forecast_pending
				if = { limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_episode_impacted } set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_history }
				remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states value = THIS }
				acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
				acid_rain_close_state_evacuation = yes
				CONTROLLER = { acid_rain_refresh_country_forecast_status = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_severe_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_severe_cells }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_severe_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_5_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_5_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
			end = acid_rain_global_severe_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_global_severe_index
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_active_entries^acid_rain_global_severe_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_severe_cell_cooldown_day_entries^acid_rain_global_severe_index = global.date }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_6_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_6_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.front_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_global_front_index
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_7_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_7_break
				array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_target_front_id value = acid_rain_global_front_index compare = equals } } acid_rain_release_front_state = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_state_entries^acid_rain_global_front_index = constant:acid_rain_front_state.retired }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_active_front_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_warning_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_8_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_8_break
			array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
			acid_rain_touch_state_for_global_layer = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
					NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_pulse_recorded }
				}
				# The transition shock is an immediate, state-keyed mortality
				# transaction. Later global pulses inherit the ordinary cadence.
				set_state_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_pulse_recorded
				set_variable = { acid_rain_last_pulse_day = global.date }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.global }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.standard }
				acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
				acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_global_opening_complete
		acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast = yes
		acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host }
			event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = {
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.112 }
				news_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.204 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_transition_receipt_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_coverage_complete
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_complete_day = global.date }
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_9_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
