		for_each_scope_loop = { break = acid_rain_begin_global_transition_loop_9_break array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries acid_rain_project_country_obligations = yes acid_rain_033_achievement_check_runtime_country = yes }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Totalen Chaos first creates a three-day warning; no new front or
# cell can be created while that warning is pending.
acid_rain_try_global_evolution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { acid_rain_global_evolution_is_enabled = yes }
		}
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
		clear_variable = global.acid_rain_global_warning_day
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.regional }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active }
		}
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_severe_transition_pending
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_front_split_pending
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.global_warning }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_warning_day = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_notice_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_global_warning_day = chaosx_date_offset }
		if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.111 } } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_enabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_global_warning_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		acid_rain_begin_global_transition = yes
	}
}

# END_EVENT033_GLOBAL_TRANSITION

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Superstorms, coverage progress, dissipation, and host scheduling
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# STATE SCOPE. Builds the bounded weighted target pool for a global
# superstorm. A state in a region without another current superstorm receives
# the largest bonus; population, logistics, and history refine the state pick.
acid_rain_append_state_to_superstorm_weighted_pool = {
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.base }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_active_entries^acid_rain_region_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.region_without_active_storm_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_population_start_k value = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.population_medium_k compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.population_medium_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_population_start_k value = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.population_large_k compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.population_large_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.logistics_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_history }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.previous_superstorm_penalty }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight
		min = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.minimum
		max = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.maximum
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_append_state_to_superstorm_weighted_pool_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_append_state_to_superstorm_weighted_pool_loop_1_break
		limit = { check_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight > constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
		add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates value = THIS }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_state_weight = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Adds one bounded, reusable-slot superstorm forecast. The target
# starts as a weighted regional seed, grows through connected states, and only
# drops adjacency when an island would otherwise prevent the minimum target.
acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_not_disabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_superstorms compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_1_break
			start = constant:acid_rain_region.first
			end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.region_loop_end
			value = acid_rain_superstorm_region_index
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_region_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_2_break
			array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_active_entries^acid_rain_region_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		set_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_target_goal min = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.target_min_states max = constant:acid_rain_superstorm_target_weight.target_max_states_exclusive integer = yes }
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_target_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		while_loop_effect = {
			break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_3_break
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets^num value = acid_rain_superstorm_target_goal compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_target_attempt value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.max_target_attempts compare = less_than }
			}
			clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_4_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_4_break
				array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
				if = {
					limit = {
						acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
						NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets value = THIS } }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
							any_neighbor_state = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets value = THIS } }
						}
					}
					acid_rain_append_state_to_superstorm_weighted_pool = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_5_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_5_break
					array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
							NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target }
							NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets value = THIS } }
						}
						acid_rain_append_state_to_superstorm_weighted_pool = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				random_scope_in_array = {
					array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_weighted_candidates
					limit = {
						acid_rain_state_is_frozen_valid = yes
						NOT = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target }
						NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets value = THIS } }
					}
					add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets value = THIS }
				}
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_target_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.max_target_attempts } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_target_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets^num value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_6_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_6_break
				start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
				end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.six
				value = acid_rain_superstorm_slot_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = acid_rain_new_superstorm_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_variable = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_slot_index }
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_slot_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two compare = equals }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = acid_rain_superstorm_slot_index }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_new_superstorm_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_total_episodes = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
				add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_ids value = acid_rain_new_superstorm_id }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_episode_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = global.acid_rain_superstorm_total_episodes }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = global.date }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.superstorm_lead_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = chaosx_date_offset }
				set_temp_variable_to_random = { var = chaosx_date_days min = constant:acid_rain_evolution.superstorm_duration_min_days max = constant:acid_rain_evolution.superstorm_duration_max_days_exclusive integer = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id }
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = chaosx_date_offset }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_count_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets^num }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_7_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_7_break
					array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_planner_targets
					set_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target
					# The seed state, listed first, names the region the map marker uses.
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
							has_variable = acid_rain_region_id
						}
						set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id = acid_rain_region_id }
					}
					set_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_pending
					set_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_forecast_reported
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_id = acid_rain_new_superstorm_id }
					clr_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_episode_impacted
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_id = global.acid_rain_superstorm_total_episodes }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_day = global.date }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_start_day = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id }
					# An evacuation started after this day cannot finish before impact.
					set_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_latest_start_day = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_start_day }
					set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_urgent_cost.evacuation_days }
					set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
					chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
					subtract_from_variable = { acid_rain_evacuation_latest_start_day = chaosx_date_offset }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_end_day = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_new_superstorm_id }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_last_superstorm_pulse_day = global.date }
					set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_opening_recorded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
					acid_rain_publish_state_country_counts = yes
					acid_rain_033_achievement_record_runtime_forecast = yes
					save_event_target_as = acid_rain_report_state
					set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forecast_state_population_k = state_population_k }
					CONTROLLER = {
						acid_rain_register_country_on_control_transfer = yes
						set_country_flag = acid_rain_country_severe_forecast_active
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_last_superstorm_forecast_report_id }
									check_variable = { var = acid_rain_last_superstorm_forecast_report_id value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_total_episodes compare = not_equals }
								}
							}
							set_variable = { acid_rain_last_superstorm_forecast_report_id = global.acid_rain_superstorm_total_episodes }
							add_to_variable = { acid_rain_severe_forecast_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forecast_report_kind = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_forecast_report_lead_days = constant:acid_rain_evolution.superstorm_lead_days }
							acid_rain_open_forecast_report = yes
						}
						acid_rain_add_state_to_forecast_report = yes
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_8_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					break = acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast_loop_8_break
					array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
					if = { limit = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target check_variable = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_id value = acid_rain_new_superstorm_id compare = equals } NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states value = THIS } } } add_to_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states value = THIS } }
				}

			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Global capacity expands after a sustained global layer, but it is
# still bounded by the absolute five-forecast registry and never bypasses the
# nine-day forecast notice.
acid_rain_try_extra_superstorm = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_not_disabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_superstorm_third_capacity compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.acid_rain_global_transition_day }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_global_age_days = chaosx_date_days }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_global_age_days value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_extra_superstorm_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_extra_superstorm_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_superstorm_third_capacity }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_capacity_three_reached
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_not_disabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_superstorm_fourth_capacity compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:acid_rain_evolution.world_collapse_superstorm_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_superstorm_fourth_capacity }
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_capacity_four_reached
	}
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_refill_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_try_extra_superstorm_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	while_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_try_extra_superstorm_loop_1_break
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
			acid_rain_global_evolution_is_not_disabled = yes
			acid_rain_runtime_is_active = yes
			check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_capacity compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_refill_attempt value = constant:acid_rain_event.max_superstorms compare = less_than }
		}
		acid_rain_create_superstorm_forecast = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_refill_attempt = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Processes each forecast on the host schedule and clears only the
# temporary target flag at retirement. Superstorm deaths remain individual
# exact population receipts, not a shared population or attrition estimate.
acid_rain_process_superstorms = {
	clear_temp_array = acid_rain_superstorm_processing_snapshot
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_each_scope_loop = { break = acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_1_break array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states add_to_temp_array = { array = acid_rain_superstorm_processing_snapshot value = THIS } }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_event.max_superstorms }
	add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_loop_end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
	set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		break = acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_2_break
		start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
		end = acid_rain_superstorm_loop_end
		value = acid_rain_superstorm_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_retiring_status = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index }
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_5_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_5_break
				array = acid_rain_superstorm_processing_snapshot
				if = {
					limit = { acid_rain_state_is_superstorm_worker_owned = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_retiring_status value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
						acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
						acid_rain_033_achievement_close_runtime_episode = yes
					}
					clr_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target
					remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states value = THIS }
					clr_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_pending
					acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
					acid_rain_close_state_evacuation = yes
					CONTROLLER = { acid_rain_refresh_country_forecast_status = yes }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.two }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_region_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
			remove_from_array = { array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_ids value = acid_rain_superstorm_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_retiring_status value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_count value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				subtract_from_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = global.date compare = less_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_3_break
				array = acid_rain_superstorm_processing_snapshot
				if = {
					limit = { acid_rain_state_is_superstorm_worker_owned = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
						set_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_superstorm_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
					}
					clr_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_pending
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_active_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_superstorm_start_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index value = global.date compare = less_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_superstorm_end_entries^acid_rain_superstorm_index compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_4_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				break = acid_rain_process_superstorms_loop_4_break
				array = acid_rain_superstorm_processing_snapshot
				if = {
					limit = { acid_rain_state_is_superstorm_worker_owned = yes }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								NOT = { has_variable = acid_rain_superstorm_opening_recorded }
								check_variable = { var = acid_rain_superstorm_opening_recorded value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals }
							}
						}
						set_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_history
						acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
						set_variable = { acid_rain_last_superstorm_pulse_day = global.date }
						set_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_opening_recorded = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme }
						acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
						acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_next_pulse_day = acid_rain_last_superstorm_pulse_day }
						set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.exposure_days }
						set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
						chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
						add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_superstorm_next_pulse_day = chaosx_date_offset }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = acid_rain_superstorm_next_pulse_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
							acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
							set_variable = { acid_rain_last_superstorm_pulse_day = global.date }
							add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_key = global.acid_rain_pulse_sequence }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_class = constant:acid_rain_mortality_class.superstorm }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_opening = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
							set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_pulse_intensity = constant:acid_rain_intensity.extreme }
							acid_rain_apply_state_pulse = yes
							acid_rain_apply_state_damage = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. World Coverage is a derived managed value from the frozen
# registry, never a second target scan.
acid_rain_refresh_world_coverage = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_world_coverage = global.acid_rain_total_touched_states }
		divide_variable = { global.acid_rain_world_coverage = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { global.acid_rain_world_coverage = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero } }
}

# ANY SCOPE. Evaluates one authored dissipation check. Ordinary failures use
# a random ten-to-twenty-day retry; global failures stay on the fourteen-day
# cadence. Atmospheric pressure can lower only non-guaranteed global checks.
acid_rain_try_dissipation_check = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending } }
		add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_guaranteed = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_one compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_one } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_two compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_two } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_three compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_three } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_four compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_four } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_five compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_five } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_six compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_six } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_seven compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_seven } }
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_eight }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_guaranteed = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_guaranteed value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_pressure_penalty = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_air_current_bp value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_air_pressure_threshold_bp compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_pressure_penalty = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_air_pressure_penalty }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_superstorm_penalty = global.acid_rain_superstorm_count }
				multiply_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_superstorm_penalty = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_superstorm_penalty_each }
				add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_pressure_penalty = acid_rain_dissipation_superstorm_penalty }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_pressure_penalty min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero max = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_pressure_penalty_cap }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = acid_rain_dissipation_pressure_penalty }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_chance min = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.minimum_nonguaranteed_chance max = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_chance_eight }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_one compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_one } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_two compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_two } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_three compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_three } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_four compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_four } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_five compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_five } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index value = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_six compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_six } }
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_chance = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_chance_seven }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_dissipation_guaranteed = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
			}
		}
		set_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_roll min = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one max = constant:acid_rain_event.probability_roll_max_exclusive integer = yes }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_last_dissipation_chance = acid_rain_dissipation_chance }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_last_dissipation_roll = acid_rain_dissipation_roll }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_guaranteed value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_roll value = acid_rain_dissipation_chance compare = less_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			acid_rain_begin_dissipation = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = global.date }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.check_interval_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable_to_random = { var = acid_rain_dissipation_retry_days min = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.failed_check_delay_min_days max = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.failed_check_delay_max_days_exclusive integer = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = acid_rain_dissipation_retry_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. A successful chance check enters one idempotent cleanup
# transaction and closes every creation path before cleanup begins.
acid_rain_begin_dissipation = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending } }
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.dissipation }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_step = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = global.date }
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. The chance ladder has already succeeded. This stored transaction
# step is safe to repeat after reload and leaves Air contamination untouched.
acid_rain_apply_dissipation_step = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_dissipation_step value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_step = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.step_one }
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_progress = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_coverage_ladder_100 }
		acid_rain_finish_runtime = yes
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Stores the worldwide summary the dissipation report shows, once,
# when acute rain closes. It only reads the event totals.
# Inputs: temporary acid_rain_unresolved_aftermath_count, counted by the final
# cleanup pass over the frozen states, and acid_rain_033_natural_closure, so
# Event Details can tell a natural dispersal from an early end.
acid_rain_store_dissipation_report_facts = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_033_natural_closure value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_closed_early
	}
	else = { set_global_flag = acid_rain_closed_early }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.date }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = global.acid_rain_runtime_start_day }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_date_offset_to_days = yes
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_report_duration_days = chaosx_date_days }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_report_air_added_percent = global.acid_rain_air_lifetime_added_bp }
	divide_variable = { global.acid_rain_report_air_added_percent = constant:acid_rain_runtime.percentage_divisor }
	set_variable = { global.acid_rain_report_unresolved_aftermath_states = acid_rain_unresolved_aftermath_count }
}

# ANY SCOPE. One final bounded cleanup is idempotent. Durable coverage,
# generation, positive-loss receipts, Event 33 totals, Air additions, and
# aftermath flags remain available to reports and achievements.
acid_rain_finish_runtime = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved } }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_natural_closure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending NOT = { has_global_flag = world_end } } set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_natural_closure = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one } }
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unresolved_aftermath_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_finish_runtime_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_finish_runtime_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_frozen_states
			acid_rain_advance_consequence_interval = yes
			acid_rain_assess_window_aftermath = yes
			if = { limit = { OR = { hidden_trigger = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target } hidden_trigger = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target } } } acid_rain_033_achievement_close_runtime_episode = yes }
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_front_target
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_cell_target
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_target
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_global_pulse_receipt
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_warning_pending
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_severe_forecast_pending
			clr_state_flag = acid_rain_superstorm_forecast_pending
			acid_rain_reconcile_state_consequences = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_core_of = OWNER check_variable = { var = acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_generation = global.acid_rain_generation_id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_state_id = THIS.id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_snapshot_receipt_key = THIS.id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_snapshot_day = global.date }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_owned_core_valid = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_033_input_snapshot_aftermath_tier = acid_rain_state_aftermath_tier }
				acid_rain_033_achievement_record_dissipation_state_snapshot = yes
			}
			acid_rain_close_state_evacuation = yes
			if = { limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_state_flag = acid_rain_aftercare_unresolved } } add_to_temp_variable = { acid_rain_unresolved_aftermath_count = constant:acid_rain_runtime.loop_increment } }
		}
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_ordinary_target_states
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_severe_target_states
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_superstorm_target_states
		clear_array = global.acid_rain_active_consequence_states
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
		acid_rain_store_dissipation_report_facts = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_finish_runtime_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_finish_runtime_loop_2_break
			array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
			acid_rain_project_country_obligations = yes
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.109 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_033_natural_closure value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } } acid_rain_033_achievement_record_acute_closure = yes }
			else = { acid_rain_033_achievement_mark_admin_disqualified = yes }
			# Natural closure keeps paid recovery work and demobilization running;
			# administrative closure releases every operation.
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_keep_recovery_operations = acid_rain_033_natural_closure }
			acid_rain_enter_country_recovery_tail = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { hidden_trigger = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_final_dissipation_receipt } } }
			set_global_flag = acid_rain_final_dissipation_receipt
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_033_natural_closure value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_receipt } }
					set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.global_final_chaos_loss }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:acid_rain_evolution.ordinary_final_chaos_loss } }
				add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host check_variable = { var = acid_rain_033_natural_closure value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } } event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = { news_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.205 } } }
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_resolved
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_endpoint_recorded
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_aftermath_retained
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_global_transition_active
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_gui_window_visible = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host }
			event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = {
				clr_country_flag = acid_rain_runtime_host
			}
			clear_global_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host
		}
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_host_worker_scheduled
		clr_global_flag = acid_rain_host_bound
		set_variable = { global.acid_rain_phase = constant:acid_rain_phase.closed }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = acid_rain_033_natural_closure value = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { global.acid_rain_world_coverage = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.global_coverage_ladder_100 }
		}
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. Called at terminal commitment before country/world rewrites.
# Uses only Event 33's frozen registries, preserves actual coverage and shared
# population/Air history, and cannot create natural-dissipation credit.
acid_rain_interrupt_for_world_end = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = world_end has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active }
		acid_rain_finish_runtime = yes
	}
}

# ANY SCOPE. This is the only scheduler entrypoint. It saves a global host
# target once and schedules a bounded country event; it never iterates the
# country list or state list to discover work.
acid_rain_bind_host_and_schedule_worker = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_host_bound } }
		save_global_event_target_as = acid_rain_runtime_host
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_host_bound
		set_country_flag = acid_rain_runtime_host
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_host_worker_scheduled }
			has_event_target = acid_rain_runtime_host
		}
		set_global_flag = acid_rain_host_worker_scheduled
		event_target:acid_rain_runtime_host = {
			acid_rain_stamp_current_callback_chain = yes
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_worker_event_days = constant:acid_rain_runtime.worker_days }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr33.20 days = acid_rain_worker_event_days }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY SCOPE. The event calls this effect on the stored host every three
# days. All work is bounded by frozen arrays and the three front/superstorm
# registries.
acid_rain_host_worker = {
	clr_global_flag = acid_rain_host_worker_scheduled
	acid_rain_interrupt_for_world_end = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active }
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_host_worker_loop_1_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_host_worker_loop_1_break
			array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
			# AI scoring and the players' decision cost text both read these rows.
			acid_rain_refresh_country_project_quotes = yes
			acid_rain_refresh_country_action_quotes = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.acid_rain_phase
					value = constant:acid_rain_phase.regional
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_host_worker_loop_2_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				break = acid_rain_host_worker_loop_2_break
				start = constant:acid_rain_runtime.one
				end = constant:acid_rain_runtime.front_loop_end
				value = acid_rain_worker_front_id
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_front_id = acid_rain_worker_front_id }
				set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_selected_region_id = global.acid_rain_front_region_entries^acid_rain_front_id }
				acid_rain_process_front = yes
				acid_rain_pulse_front_states = yes
			}
			acid_rain_process_severe_cells = yes
			acid_rain_try_severe_evolution = yes
			acid_rain_try_multiple_fronts = yes
			acid_rain_try_global_evolution = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.acid_rain_phase
					value = constant:acid_rain_phase.global_warning
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			acid_rain_try_global_evolution = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
				check_variable = {
					var = global.acid_rain_phase
					value = constant:acid_rain_phase.global
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			acid_rain_process_superstorms = yes
			acid_rain_pulse_global_states = yes
			acid_rain_try_extra_superstorm = yes
		}
		acid_rain_try_weekly_air_request = yes
		acid_rain_refresh_active_consequences = yes
		set_temp_variable = { acid_rain_host_worker_loop_3_break = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			break = acid_rain_host_worker_loop_3_break
			array = global.acid_rain_participant_countries
			acid_rain_project_country_obligations = yes
			acid_rain_033_achievement_check_runtime_country = yes
			# A player's open world map shows that player's own national strip, so
			# refresh it here; the host refresh below projects the shared world view.
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					has_country_flag = acid_rain_gui_window_open
					has_variable = acid_rain_gui_view_generation
					check_variable = { var = acid_rain_gui_view_generation value = global.acid_rain_generation_id compare = equals }
				}
				acid_rain_gui_refresh_national_strip = yes
			}
		}
		acid_rain_refresh_world_coverage = yes
		acid_rain_gui_refresh_read_only = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active
				NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active }
				acid_rain_all_regions_are_complete = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_coverage_complete } }
				set_global_flag = acid_rain_coverage_complete
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_coverage_complete_day = global.date }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_dissipation_check_index = constant:acid_rain_runtime.zero }
				set_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = global.date }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:acid_rain_dissipation.ordinary_completion_wait_days }
				set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
				chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
				add_to_variable = { global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day = chaosx_date_offset }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				acid_rain_try_dissipation_check = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_global_layer_active
				NOT = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_global_minimum_dissipation_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.acid_rain_total_touched_states value = global.acid_rain_total_valid_states compare = equals }
			}
			acid_rain_try_dissipation_check = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = acid_rain_dissipation_pending
				check_variable = { var = global.date value = global.acid_rain_next_dissipation_check_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			acid_rain_apply_dissipation_step = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = acid_rain_runtime_active }
			acid_rain_bind_host_and_schedule_worker = yes
		}
	}
}

# END_EVENT033_HOST_RUNTIME
